+// A SHAPE TOKEN NOBODY ISSUED IS TEXT, but for its shape (found by test-tokens-edges-volume, 2026-11-18: any
+// token-SHAPED span was kept whole, issued or not, so what stood in its free slots was never looked at - a 9-digit SSN
+// as a shortened shape's length, #0...123456789#abcdefg; seven digits of a phone or a name as the id, #0#8675309,
+// #X#zqhavis). One this masker issued (or the store has, or a worked example's) is kept whole as before; of any other
+// only the shape is kept - X x 0 and separators, nothing of a value - and its length and id are judged as any text is
+// (but in what the developer typed: maskText).
+// (the store is asked again only when it has changed: a typed token in the conversation is in every request)
+let _takenAtVer = null;
+function psTakeNewValuesIfChanged() {
+  const v = psVault();
+  if (!v) return false;
+  let ver = null; try { ver = v.version(); } catch { return false; }
+  if (ver !== null && ver === _takenAtVer) return false;
+  _takenAtVer = ver;
+  return psTakeNewValues();
+}
+const SHORT_SHAPE_HEAD = /^([Xx0][-./:␣Xx0]{0,23}(?:…|[.]{3}))[1-9][0-9]{0,8}$/;
+// the ids this masker holds a value for, rebuilt when it holds more (resolve() walks every value for an id it does not
+// have: 5,000 near-miss tokens in a megabyte, each asked, went past the worker's limit)
+let _idsOf = null, _idsSize = -1, _idsMap = null;
+function issuedId(m, id) {
+  const vals = m._values;
+  if (_idsMap !== vals || _idsSize !== vals.size) {
+    _idsOf = new Set();
+    for (const k of vals.keys()) { const i = k.indexOf('|'); _idsOf.add(i < 0 ? k : k.slice(0, i)); }
+    _idsMap = vals; _idsSize = vals.size;
+  }
+  return _idsOf.has(id);
+}
+function tokenKeepSpan(m, t, state) {
+  const [tok, desc, id] = t;
+  const whole = [t.index, t.index + tok.length];
+  if (desc.startsWith('@') || EXAMPLE_IDS.has(id) || state.typed) return whole;   // a named token: code_boss validated it before this filter
+  const short = SHORT_SHAPE_HEAD.exec(desc);
+  const issued = () => { if (!issuedId(m, id)) return false; const h = m.resolve(tok); return !!h && (h.exact || !short); };
+  if (issued()) return whole;
+  if (!state.took) { state.took = true; if (psTakeNewValuesIfChanged() && issued()) return whole; }
+  const descAt = t.index + tok.length - id.length - 1 - desc.length;
+  return [t.index, descAt + (short ? short[1].length : desc.length)];
+}
+// A WORD WRITTEN WITH \uXXXX ESCAPES IS JUDGED AS IT READS (found by test-tokens-edges-volume, 2026-11-18: in a JSON
+// file written ASCII-only - Python's json.dumps default - "Müller-Qorväth" reached the model whole, and of
+// "李小龙" every piece was shown as an escape: read run by run, an escape's letters are "u00fc", a piece
+// of a word no list knows the way it knows the word). Each word with an escape in it is decoded, and unless the word it
+// spells is one this masker shows, every run of it is hidden. Never shown more than before: only hidden more.
+const WORD_CHAR = /^[\p{L}\p{N}\p{M}]$/u;
+const U_ESCAPE = /\\u[0-9a-fA-F]{4}/g;
+function escapedWordSpans(text, m) {
+  const out = [];
+  const escAt = (i) => {
+    if (i < 0 || text[i] !== '\\' || text[i + 1] !== 'u') return null;
+    const h = text.slice(i + 2, i + 6);
+    if (!/^[0-9a-fA-F]{4}$/.test(h)) return null;
+    const c = String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16));
+    return WORD_CHAR.test(c) ? c : null;
+  };
+  let end = 0;
+  U_ESCAPE.lastIndex = 0;
+  for (let u; (u = U_ESCAPE.exec(text));) {
+    if (u.index < end || !escAt(u.index)) continue;
+    let s = u.index, e = u.index;
+    for (;;) { if (escAt(s - 6)) s -= 6; else if (s > 0 && WORD_CHAR.test(text[s - 1])) s--; else break; }
+    for (;;) { if (escAt(e)) e += 6; else if (e < text.length && WORD_CHAR.test(text[e])) e++; else break; }
+    end = e;
+    const word = text.slice(s, e).replace(/\\u([0-9a-fA-F]{4})/g, (all, h) => { const c = String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16)); return WORD_CHAR.test(c) ? c : all; });
+    if (!escShown(m, word)) out.push([s, e]);
+  }
+  return out;
+}
+const inSpans = (spans, s, e) => {
+  let lo = 0, hi = spans.length;
+  while (lo < hi) { const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1; if (spans[mid][1] <= s) lo = mid + 1; else hi = mid; }
+  return lo < spans.length && spans[lo][0] < e;
+};

+// "Inside the repo" by the REAL path, not the spelled one: path.resolve + a prefix check let a junction (or symlink)
+// inside the repo that points outside it through, and the file outside was rewritten (found by test-api-edges-volume
+// 2026-11-18). Both the repo root and the target are resolved with realpath. A target that is not there passes on
+// the spelled path (the read after this fails, so nothing is written); any other failure to resolve is refused.
+function gcInsideRepo(repoDir, abs) {
+  const lexRoot = path.resolve(repoDir);
+  if (!abs.toLowerCase().startsWith(lexRoot.toLowerCase() + path.sep)) return false;
+  const real = (p) => { try { return realpathSync.native(p); } catch { return realpathSync(p); } };
+  let root, target;
+  try { root = real(lexRoot); } catch { return false; }
+  try { target = real(abs); } catch (e) { return !!(e && e.code === 'ENOENT'); }
+  const r = root.toLowerCase(), t = target.toLowerCase();
+  return t.startsWith(r.endsWith(path.sep) ? r : r + path.sep);
+}

+// `fn` applied to the text up to its last ">" only, the rest kept as is: for a replace whose every match ends in ">"
+// (found by test-parse-edges-volume, 2026-11-18: TURN_TAGS_RE's `[^>]*>` was retried from every "<waiting " with no
+// ">" after it, each try running to the end - O(n^2); past the last ">" no match can end, so the result is the same)
+function beforeLastGt(text, fn) {
+  const g = text.lastIndexOf('>');
+  return g < 0 ? text : fn(text.slice(0, g + 1)) + text.slice(g + 1);
+}
+// Trailing horizontal rule lines, peeled off the end one line at a time: the same cut as
+// /(?:(?:^|\r?\n)[ \t]*([-*_])(?:[ \t]*\1){2,}[ \t]*)+$/ (found 2026-11-18: that regex, retried from every rule line
+// that was not followed by rules only, took 2,500 "---" lines mid-text O(n^2) - 0.14 s for 10 KB)
+const RULE_ONLY_LINE = /^[ \t]*([-*_])(?:[ \t]*\1){2,}[ \t]*$/;
+function stripTrailingRules(s) {
+  let end = s.length, cut = -1;
+  for (;;) {
+    const nl = s.lastIndexOf('\n', end - 1);
+    if (!RULE_ONLY_LINE.test(s.slice(nl + 1, end))) break;
+    if (nl < 0) { cut = 0; break; }
+    cut = s[nl - 1] === '\r' ? nl - 1 : nl;
+    end = cut;
+  }
+  return cut < 0 ? s : s.slice(0, cut);
+}

+// <js followed by a non-name character (or the end): the `<js\b` of the old regex
+const JS_START_RE = /<js(?![A-Za-z0-9_])/gi;
+/**
+ * Where the open tag of a snippet ends: the index after its ">" - the first ">" outside a quoted value, a quote
+ * running to the same quote char (no escapes, as the regex it replaces) - or -1 when there is none (a quote never
+ * closed, or no ">" at or before `lastGt`). `j` is just past "<js".
+ */
+export function jsOpenTagEnd(s, j, lastGt = s.length - 1) {
+  for (; j <= lastGt; j++) {
+    const c = s[j];
+    if (c === '>') return j + 1;
+    if (c === '"' || c === "'") { const k = s.indexOf(c, j + 1); if (k < 0) return -1; j = k; }
+  }
+  return -1;
+}
+
+/**
+ * THE BLOCK'S OWN END, FOUND THE WAY parseToolCalls READS THE BLOCK (found by test-parse-edges-volume, 2026-11-18: the
+ * reply parser and the live pump both found a block's </remote-workspace> - and the result-tag cut - on the raw text,
+ * before the snippet bodies were carved out, so a snippet writing "</remote-workspace>" in a string had its block cut
+ * there and its call marked __unterminated - 7 times in one live session - and one grepping for "<workspace-result"
+ * had its block dropped to prose; this file's header promised neither could happen).
+ *
+ * An incremental scanner over a block body: snippets (<js ...>...</js>, the carving rule above) are skipped whole, and
+ * what is left is searched for the block's </remote-workspace> or a result tag. Feed it the text as it grows
+ * (`next(get, len, atEof)`); it never rescans what it has passed. `get(a, b)` returns the text's [a, b) slice.
+ * Results: { kind: 'close', at, end } | { kind: 'result', at } | { kind: 'wait' } (more text needed) |
+ * { kind: 'none' } (atEof, neither found). `snips` holds the [start, end) of the snippets passed (the newest 32).
+ * A snippet whose </js> never comes protects nothing: at the end the scan goes back over it as plain text, as the
+ * carving does (no </js> after it means no later snippet can close either).
+ */
+export function createBlockScanner(bodyStart, { keepAll = false } = {}) {
+  const st = { pos: bodyStart, snipOpen: -1, snipBody: -1, noSnips: false, snips: [] };
+  const CAND = /<(?:js(?![A-Za-z0-9_])|\/remote-workspace>|remote-workspace-result|workspace-result)/gi;
+  const CLOSE = /<\/js\s*>/gi;
+  const PARTIALS = ['<js', '</remote-workspace>', '<remote-workspace-result', '<workspace-result'];
+  const isPartial = (t) => { const l = t.toLowerCase(); return PARTIALS.some((p) => p.length > l.length && p.startsWith(l)); };
+  st.next = (get, len, atEof) => {
+    for (;;) {
+      if (st.snipBody >= 0) {
+        const w = get(st.pos, len);
+        CLOSE.lastIndex = 0;
+        const m = CLOSE.exec(w);
+        if (m) {
+          const end = st.pos + m.index + m[0].length;
+          st.snips.push([st.snipOpen, end]); if (!keepAll && st.snips.length > 32) st.snips.shift();
+          st.pos = end; st.snipOpen = -1; st.snipBody = -1;
+          continue;
+        }
+        if (atEof) { st.noSnips = true; st.pos = st.snipBody; st.snipOpen = -1; st.snipBody = -1; continue; }
+        // resume where a close could still be starting: a trailing "</js" + whitespace, or a partial "</j"
+        let r = w.length;
+        while (r > 0 && /\s/.test(w[r - 1])) r--;
+        let keep = w.length;
+        if (r >= 4 && /^<\/js$/i.test(w.slice(r - 4, r))) keep = r - 4;
+        else if (r === w.length) { for (let k = Math.min(3, w.length); k >= 1; k--) if (/^<(?:\/j?)?$/i.test(w.slice(w.length - k))) { keep = w.length - k; break; } }
+        st.pos += keep;
+        return { kind: 'wait' };
+      }
+      const w = get(st.pos, len);
+      CAND.lastIndex = 0;
+      let m;
+      for (;;) {
+        m = CAND.exec(w);
+        if (!m) break;
+        if (m[0].length === 3 && st.noSnips) { CAND.lastIndex = m.index + 1; continue; }
+        break;
+      }
+      if (!m) {
+        if (!atEof) {
+          const lt = w.lastIndexOf('<');
+          if (lt >= 0 && w.length - lt < 25 && isPartial(w.slice(lt))) { st.pos += lt; return { kind: 'wait' }; }
+        }
+        st.pos += w.length;
+        return atEof ? { kind: 'none' } : { kind: 'wait' };
+      }
+      const at = st.pos + m.index;
+      const tag = m[0].toLowerCase();
+      if (tag === '</remote-workspace>') { st.pos = at + m[0].length; return { kind: 'close', at, end: at + m[0].length }; }
+      if (tag !== '<js') { st.pos = at; return { kind: 'result', at }; }
+      if (!atEof && m.index + 3 === w.length) { st.pos = at; return { kind: 'wait' }; }   // "<js" at the end: "<jsx" yet?
+      const openEnd = jsOpenTagEnd(w, m.index + 3);
+      if (openEnd < 0) {
+        if (!atEof) { st.pos = at; return { kind: 'wait' }; }
+        st.pos = at + 1;
+        continue;
+      }
+      st.snipOpen = at; st.snipBody = st.pos + openEnd; st.pos = st.snipBody;
+    }
+  };
+  return st;
+}
+/** Is `at` inside a snippet the scanner has passed, or inside the one it is in? */
+export function inScannedSnippet(st, at) {
+  if (!st) return false;
+  if (st.snipBody >= 0 && at >= st.snipBody) return true;
+  for (const [a, b] of st.snips) if (at >= a && at < b) return true;
+  return false;
+}

+  // decides it: it matches exactly when some ">" follows it, and if none follows it none follows a later one)

+/** True when the file exists, is non-empty and its last byte is not '\n' (a torn last line). */
+export function endsWithoutNewline(file) {
+  let fd = null;
+  try {
+    const size = statSync(file).size;
+    if (size <= 0) return false;
+    fd = openSync(file, 'r');
+    const b = Buffer.alloc(1);
+    readSync(fd, b, 0, 1, size - 1);
+    return b[0] !== 0x0a;
+  } catch { return false; } finally { if (fd != null) try { closeSync(fd); } catch {} }
+}

+/**
+ * A reply's text while it streams, in pieces (see runTurnStreaming, 2026-11-18): appending never copies, and a read
+ * copies only what it reads - the newest ~1 KB is one growing string (`tail`), everything before it finished flat
+ * pieces. `len`, `slice(a, b)`, `append(d)`, `reset(s)`, `str()` (the whole text, built once), and the positions of
+ * the last two newlines (lastNl/prevNl, -1 when none) for the footer checks.
+ */
+export function createTextBuf() {
+  const parts = [], starts = [];
+  let head = 0, tail = '', lastNl = -1, prevNl = -1;
+  const TAIL_MAX = 1024;
+  const B = {
+    len: 0,
+    append(d) {
+      if (!d) return;
+      const base = B.len;
+      for (let k = d.indexOf('\n'); k >= 0; k = d.indexOf('\n', k + 1)) { prevNl = lastNl; lastNl = base + k; }
+      tail += d; B.len += d.length;
+      if (tail.length >= TAIL_MAX) { parts.push(tail); starts.push(head); head += tail.length; tail = ''; }
+    },
+    reset(s) {
+      s = String(s ?? '');
+      parts.length = 0; starts.length = 0; head = 0; tail = ''; B.len = 0; lastNl = -1; prevNl = -1;
+      if (!s) return;
+      parts.push(s); starts.push(0); head = s.length; B.len = s.length;
+      lastNl = s.lastIndexOf('\n'); prevNl = lastNl > 0 ? s.lastIndexOf('\n', lastNl - 1) : -1;
+    },
+    slice(a = 0, b = B.len) {
+      a = Math.max(0, Math.min(a, B.len)); b = Math.max(a, Math.min(b, B.len));
+      if (a === b) return '';
+      if (a >= head) return tail.slice(a - head, b - head);
+      let lo = 0, hi = parts.length - 1;
+      while (lo < hi) { const mid = (lo + hi + 1) >> 1; if (starts[mid] <= a) lo = mid; else hi = mid - 1; }
+      let out = '';
+      for (let i = lo; i < parts.length && starts[i] < b; i++) out += parts[i].slice(Math.max(0, a - starts[i]), b - starts[i]);
+      if (b > head) out += tail.slice(0, b - head);
+      return out;
+    },
+    str() { const s = parts.length === 1 && !tail ? parts[0] : parts.join('') + tail; B.reset(s); return s; },
+    lastNl: () => lastNl,
+    prevNl: () => prevNl,
+  };
+  return B;
+}
+// findFollowupFooter(text + d, text.length - 100), read off a window: from the start of the line holding the point 100
+// characters back. A line longer than 4 KB there cannot hold a footer opener at its start (the opener is at most a few
+// characters past a line start, and would have been seen when it came), so the window then starts at the next line.
+function footerInWindow(T, d) {
+  const p = Math.max(0, T.len - 100);
+  let start;
+  if (p === 0) start = 0;
+  else if (T.lastNl() < p) start = T.lastNl() + 1;
+  else if (T.prevNl() < p) start = T.prevNl() + 1;
+  else {
+    start = -1;
+    for (let span = 128; ; span *= 4) {   // lines are short: look back a little first
+      const lo = Math.max(0, p - Math.min(span, 4096)), k = T.slice(lo, p).lastIndexOf('\n');
+      if (k >= 0) { start = lo + k + 1; break; }
+      if (lo === 0) { start = 0; break; }
+      if (span >= 4096) break;
+    }
+  }
+  if (start >= 0 && p - start > 4096) start = -1;
+  if (start < 0) {   // no line start within reach: only whole lines after p can hold one
+    const w = T.slice(p) + d, k = w.indexOf('\n');
+    if (k < 0) return -1;
+    const r = findFollowupFooter(w, k + 1);
+    return r < 0 ? -1 : p + r;
+  }
+  const r = findFollowupFooter(T.slice(start) + d, 0);
+  return r < 0 ? -1 : start + r;
+}

+// is `at` inside one of the sorted, disjoint [a, b) ranges?
+function inSorted(ranges, at) {
+  let lo = 0, hi = ranges.length - 1;
+  while (lo <= hi) { const mid = (lo + hi) >> 1, [a, b] = ranges[mid]; if (at < a) hi = mid - 1; else if (at >= b) lo = mid + 1; else return true; }
+  return false;

+  // Parse the text of a .jsonl event log; blank/torn lines are skipped (a crash can tear the last appended line -
+  // everything before it is intact and kept). A UTF-8 BOM in front of the first line is dropped by trim().
+  function parseConversationText(raw) {
+    const out = []; let lineCount = 0;
+    for (const line of String(raw).split('\n')) {
+      const s = line.trim();
+      if (!s) continue;
+      lineCount++;
+      try { const ev = JSON.parse(s); if (ev && ev.id) out.push(ev); } catch { /* torn/corrupt line — skip */ }
+    }
+    return { events: out, lineCount };
+  }
+  // A rename over a file another process has open (an editor, a tail, AV) fails on Windows with EPERM/EBUSY for a
+  // moment. The conversation's full rewrite had no retry, and flushOnShutdown calls it once: one failed rename there
+  // lost every event not yet on disk (found by test-persistence-edges-volume 2026-11-18). ~1 s in all, like
+  // writeFileAtomicSync (which uses this too).
+  function renameRetrySync(from, to) {
+    for (let i = 0; ; i++) {
+      try { renameSync(from, to); return; }
+      catch (e) { if (i >= 8 || !/^(EPERM|EBUSY|EACCES)$/.test(String(e && e.code))) throw e; Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, 30 * (i + 1)); }
+    }
+  }
+  /**
+   * THE SAVED CONVERSATION COULD NOT BE READ ON LOAD (a lock held by another process, permissions): session._cvLivePending.
+   * What the chat shows then is an older copy (.bak) or nothing, and the promise on screen is "the file has not been
+   * touched". It was broken by the next save: _cvFlushedLen was 0, so it was a FULL rewrite of what memory held
+   * over the real file - a 100-event conversation replaced by its 10-event .bak, a 40-event one by the single message
+   * typed under the banner (found by test-persistence-edges-volume 2026-11-18). Now every save first tries the real
+   * file again: still unreadable -> it is not written at all (what was added since the load goes to
+   * conversation.jsonl.pending beside it, so a shutdown cannot lose it); readable -> it is loaded, what was added
+   * since the load goes on after it (ids clear of it), and the chat is shown the real conversation again.
+   * Returns 'held' (not written, and the new events could not be kept beside it either), 'held-safe' (not written; the
+   * new events are in the .pending file), 'merged' (session.chatEvents is now the real conversation + the new events; the
+   * normal append follows), or 'replace' (the real file is there but holds nothing that parses: kept aside as
+   * .corrupt-<ts>, memory is written whole).
+   */
+  function liveConversationMerge(jsonl) {
+    const pend = session._cvLivePending;
+    let live = null, err = null;
+    try { live = parseConversationText(readFileSync(jsonl, 'utf8')); }
+    catch (e) { if (e && e.code === 'ENOENT') live = { events: [], lineCount: 0 }; else err = e; }
+    const fresh = session.chatEvents.slice(Math.min(pend.loadedLen, session.chatEvents.length));
+    if (err) {
+      if (fresh.length !== pend.sideLen) {
+        try {
+          if (fresh.length) writeFileAtomicSync(jsonl + '.pending', fresh.map((e) => JSON.stringify(e)).join('\n') + '\n');
+          pend.sideLen = fresh.length;
+        } catch (e2) { if (!pend.sideWarned) { pend.sideWarned = true; logError('persist', e2, { op: 'conversation-pending-side-file', inMemory: fresh.length }); } }
+      }
+      if (!pend.heldLogged) { pend.heldLogged = true; fileLog(`conversation.jsonl still cannot be read (${err.code || err.message}); not written - new events are kept beside it in conversation.jsonl.pending`); }
+      // on disk beside it (held-safe): the save counts as done, so a project switch / close / shutdown goes ahead;
+      // loadConversation adds the .pending events after the real ones the next time the file can be read
+      return pend.sideLen === fresh.length ? 'held-safe' : 'held';
+    }
+    session._cvLivePending = null;
+    try { unlinkSync(jsonl + '.pending'); } catch { /* none written */ }
+    if (live.events.length === 0 && live.lineCount > 0) {
+      const stamp = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, '-');
+      try { copyFileSync(jsonl, jsonl + '.corrupt-' + stamp); } catch (e) { logError('persist', e, { op: 'preserve-corrupt-conversation' }); session._cvLivePending = pend; return 'held'; }
+      logError('persist', `conversation.jsonl readable again but nothing in it parses - kept as .corrupt-${stamp}; the conversation shown is written`, { op: 'live-merge-corrupt', lineCount: live.lineCount });
+      session._cvFlushedLen = 0; session._cvNeedFullRewrite = true;
+      return 'replace';
+    }
+    const have = new Set(live.events.map((e) => e.id));
+    let next = nextIdFor({ nextEventId: session.nextEventId, chatEvents: live.events });
+    for (const e of fresh) if (e && have.has(e.id)) e.id = 'e' + next++;   // (a backstop: the load already numbered past it)
+    session.chatEvents = live.events.concat(fresh);
+    session.nextEventId = nextIdFor({ nextEventId: next, chatEvents: session.chatEvents });
+    session._cvFlushedLen = live.events.length; session._cvNeedFullRewrite = false;
+    session.conversationUnreadable = '';
+    fileLog(`conversation.jsonl readable again: ${live.events.length} saved event(s) loaded, ${fresh.length} added since the load go on after them`);
+    logInfo('persist', 'saved conversation readable again - restored', { saved: live.events.length, added: fresh.length, shown: pend.loadedLen });
+    try {
+      broadcast({ type: 'persist-recovered' });
+      broadcast({ type: 'reset', version: bumpVersion() });
+      for (const ev of session.chatEvents) broadcast({ type: 'event', version: bumpVersion(), event: ev });
+    } catch { /* no page */ }
+    return 'merged';
+  }

+/**
+ * Is `format` a format this value can have - one a named token for it was issued with? (found by
+ * test-tokens-edges-volume, 2026-11-18: the format slot of a known named token was never checked, so
+ * #@p.last~JonasKowalczyk#<its id> passed every filter whole, a name in the slot.) True when the value laid out in it
+ * gives back exactly that format: every X x 0 is one of the value's letters or digits, every other character a
+ * separator or an escape the value's spellings have. A reference or a secret has no format.
+ */
+export function formatFits(value, format, kind = 'value') {
+  if (format == null) return true;
+  if (kind === 'ref' || kind === 'secret') return false;
+  try { return tokenFormatOf(valueInFormat(value, format)) === String(format); } catch { return false; }
+}
+/** A format holding nothing a value's format cannot: no letter but X / x, no digit but 0 (its %XX escapes aside). For a token whose value is not at hand. */
+export const formatLooksIssued = (format) => format == null || !/[\p{L}\p{N}]/u.test(String(format).replace(/%[0-9A-F]{2}/g, '').replace(/[Xx0]/g, ''));

+const counts = new Map(); profile.samples.forEach((s, i) => counts.set(s, (counts.get(s) || 0) + (profile.timeDeltas[i] || 0)));

+  const seen = new Set(); let x = nid; while (x) { const k = key(byId.get(x)); if (!seen.has(k)) { incl.set(k, (incl.get(k) || 0) + us); seen.add(k); } x = parent.get(x); }
+}
+const top = (m, n) => [...m].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, n).map(([k, us]) => String(Math.round(us / 1000)).padStart(7) + ' ms  ' + k).join('\n');

=== add test/test-api-edges-volume.mjs
new d42f6eeef85dd087c898b3ae
+/**
+ * THE HTTP API AT ITS EDGES, AND UNDER VOLUME. The real server in-process, a scripted mock adapter (no network, no
+ * browser), a temp CB_HOME and a temp git project. What it asks of the API boundary:
+ *   [1] malformed requests on every POST route: invalid JSON, JSON of the wrong type (array/number/null/string), {},
+ *       an empty body, the wrong method, a 20 MB body. Contract: an answer (no hang - every fetch has a timeout), not a
+ *       5xx, no stack trace in the body (server.mjs outer catch: "WITHOUT the raw stack reaching the browser"), and
+ *       /api/health still answers afterwards. (A 2xx for a route whose body is optional is fine; routes that take
+ *       invalid JSON as {} are listed, not failed.)
+ *   [2] paths: `..`, absolute paths outside the project, UNC, unicode + spaces, very long paths, NUL, malformed
+ *       percent-encoding - on list-dir / make-dir / open-project (the folder picker: browsing anywhere is what they
+ *       are FOR - only no 5xx / no hang / no stalled event loop is asked there) and on git-match-eol / git-revert-ws
+ *       (gcMatchRepoEol / gcRevertWhitespace: "not a file of this repo" - nothing outside the repo is written)
+ *   [3] the message flow under stress: 30 messages back-to-back during a run (via /api/message: 409; via /api/steer:
+ *       each one the server says ok to must reach the model), cancel mid-stream x20, 20 concurrent cancels,
+ *       clear-conversation during a run (409, the run unharmed), 2 SSE clients + 10 more connecting/disconnecting
+ *       rapidly (windows count back to 2, both still get frames), a stalled SSE reader, a 2 MB reply in 1-byte chunks,
+ *       a reply of 0 chunks, an adapter that throws mid-stream, one that hangs (honouring the abort signal, and not)
+ *   [4] VOLUME: 300 sequential messages - status done at the end, one user-message + one reply each, timings + memory
+ * Run: node test/test-api-edges-volume.mjs        (CB_EDGE_ONLY=1,2,3,4 runs some sections)
+ */
+import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync, existsSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import { execSync } from 'node:child_process';
+import http from 'node:http';
+import path from 'node:path';
+
+const HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-edges-home-'));
+const PROJ = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-edges-proj-'));
+const OUT = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-edges-outside-'));   // a folder outside the project, to check nothing reaches it
+process.env.CB_HOME = HOME;
+process.env.CB_SKILLS_MAINTAINER = '0';
+process.env.CB_BOOKKEEPER = '0';
+process.env.CB_HEADS_UP = '0';
+process.env.CB_HEADS_UP_IDLE_MS = '0';
+process.env.CB_SESSION_TRANSPORT = '0';
+process.env.CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS = '0';   // (piishape's masking is not what this measures; ~10 s/MB would swamp the timings)
+process.env.CB_GIT_SCAN_MS = '100000';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const failures = [];
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; failures.push(l); console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const note = (l) => console.log('  note', l);
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const ONLY = new Set(String(process.env.CB_EDGE_ONLY || '1,2,3,4').split(',').map((s) => s.trim()));
+// the whole run is bounded: whatever happens, it ends
+const WATCHDOG = setTimeout(() => { console.log(`\nWATCHDOG: the test ran past 25 minutes\n${pass} passed, ${fail + 1} failed`); process.exit(3); }, 25 * 60 * 1000);
+
+// ── the project: a git repo with files the git routes can be pointed at ──
+writeFileSync(path.join(PROJ, 'README.md'), '# edges\n');
+writeFileSync(path.join(PROJ, 'lf.txt'), 'one\ntwo\nthree\n');
+mkdirSync(path.join(PROJ, 'dir with space'), { recursive: true });
+writeFileSync(path.join(PROJ, 'dir with space', 'ünï cødé.txt'), 'alpha\nbeta\ngamma\n');
+mkdirSync(path.join(PROJ, 'lnk'), { recursive: true });
+writeFileSync(path.join(PROJ, 'lnk', 'victim.txt'), 'v1\nv2\nv3\n');
+execSync('git init -q && git config user.email t@t && git config user.name t && git config core.autocrlf false && git add -A && git commit -qm base', { cwd: PROJ, shell: true });
+// outside: a file git-match-eol / git-revert-ws must never touch (CRLF, so "matching the repo's LF" would change it)
+const OUTSIDE_FILE = path.join(OUT, 'outside.txt');
+const OUTSIDE_BYTES = 'one\r\ntwo\r\nthree\r\n';
+writeFileSync(OUTSIDE_FILE, OUTSIDE_BYTES);
+writeFileSync(path.join(OUT, 'victim.txt'), 'v1\r\nv2\r\nv3\r\n');
+
+// ── the mock adapter: a queue of behaviours, one per completeStream call; the default replies at once ──
+const A = {
+  queue: [], calls: 0, record: false, seen: [], firstChunkAt: 0, inflight: 0, hungForever: 0,
+  defaultReply: () => 'ack.',
+};
+const chunk = (s) => ({ choices: [{ delta: { content: s } }] });
+const abortErr = () => { const e = new Error('aborted'); e.name = 'AbortError'; return e; };
+const waitAbortable = (ms, signal) => new Promise((res, rej) => {
+  if (signal?.aborted) return rej(abortErr());
+  const t = setTimeout(res, ms);
+  signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', () => { clearTimeout(t); rej(abortErr()); }, { once: true });
+});
+const B_ = {
+  text: (t, size = 40, delayMs = 0) => async function* (o) { for (let i = 0; i < t.length; i += size) { if (delayMs) await waitAbortable(delayMs, o.signal); if (o.signal?.aborted) return; A.firstChunkAt ||= Date.now(); yield chunk(t.slice(i, i + size)); } },
+  bytes: (n) => async function* (o) {
+    const line = 'lorem ipsum dolor sit amet consectetur adipiscing elit sed do eiusmod tempor ut\n';   // 80 chars
+    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { if ((i & 0xff) === 0) { if (o.signal?.aborted) return; await new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r)); } A.firstChunkAt ||= Date.now(); A.bytesOut = i + 1; yield chunk(line[i % 80]); }
+  },
+  empty: () => async function* () { /* no chunks at all */ },
+  throwMid: () => async function* () { A.firstChunkAt ||= Date.now(); yield chunk('a partial answer that '); yield chunk('is cut off by '); throw Object.assign(new Error('mock adapter blew up mid-stream'), { code: 'EMOCK' }); },
+  hangHonor: () => async function* (o) { await new Promise((res, rej) => { if (o.signal?.aborted) return rej(abortErr()); o.signal?.addEventListener?.('abort', () => rej(abortErr()), { once: true }); }); },
+  hangIgnore: () => async function* () { A.hungForever++; await new Promise(() => {}); },
+};
+const adapter = {
+  async complete() { return { choices: [{ message: { content: 'ok' } }] }; },
+  async *completeStream(opts) {
+    A.calls++; A.inflight++;
+    if (A.record) { try { A.seen.push(JSON.stringify(opts?.messages || [])); } catch { A.seen.push(''); } }
+    const b = A.queue.length ? A.queue.shift() : B_.text(A.defaultReply());
+    try { yield* b(opts || {}); } finally { A.inflight--; }
+  },
+  async listModels() { return [{ id: 'm' }]; },
+};

+const { startServer } = await import('../src/server.mjs');
+const PORT = Number(process.env.CB_EDGE_PORT) || 29871;
+const { server } = await startServer({ html: '<!doctype html><title>edges</title>', baseURL: '(mock)', apiKey: 'k', gitlabCreds: { user: 't', token: 't' }, defaultModel: 'm', port: PORT, adapter, modelsList: [{ id: 'm' }], initialProject: PROJ, onStop: () => { note('(the server was asked to stop - onStop kept it running)'); } });
+const B = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;
+
+// every request has a timeout; the answer is { status, text, json, ms } or { status: 0, err }
+async function call(method, p, body, { timeout = 15000, raw = false, headers = {} } = {}) {
+  const t0 = Date.now(); const ac = new AbortController(); const t = setTimeout(() => ac.abort(), timeout);
+  try {
+    const o = { method, signal: ac.signal, headers: { ...headers } };
+    if (body !== undefined) { o.body = raw ? body : JSON.stringify(body); if (!o.headers['content-type']) o.headers['content-type'] = 'application/json'; }
+    const r = await fetch(B + p, o);
+    const text = await r.text();
+    let json = null; try { json = JSON.parse(text); } catch {}
+    return { status: r.status, text, json, ms: Date.now() - t0 };
+  } catch (e) { return { status: 0, err: e.name === 'AbortError' ? 'TIMEOUT' : (e.cause?.code || e.message), text: '', json: null, ms: Date.now() - t0 }; }
+  finally { clearTimeout(t); }
+}
+const post = (p, body, o) => call('POST', p, body, o);
+const get = (p, o) => call('GET', p, undefined, o);
+const health = async () => (await get('/api/health', { timeout: 5000 })).status === 200;
+const st = async () => (await get('/api/state?limit=1000000', { timeout: 20000 })).json || {};
+const STACK_RE = /\n\s+at\s+\S+.*\(?[A-Za-z]:?[\\/].*:\d+:\d+\)?|file:\/\/\/.*\.mjs:\d+/;
+const until = async (fn, ms = 10000, every = 25) => { const t0 = Date.now(); while (Date.now() - t0 < ms) { const v = await fn(); if (v) return v; await sleep(every); } return null; };
+const idleish = (s) => s && s.status !== 'running';
+const waitIdle = (ms = 15000) => until(async () => { const s = (await get('/api/state?limit=1')).json; return idleish(s) ? s : null; }, ms);
+const evs = (s) => (s && (s.events || s.chatEvents)) || [];
+const ensureProject = async () => { const s = (await get('/api/state?limit=1')).json; if (!s || s.activeProject !== PROJ) { await waitIdle(); await post('/api/open-project', { path: PROJ }); } };
+// a message sent; a 409 "cancelling - retry" retried as the client does
+async function send(text, { retries = 40 } = {}) {
+  let r;
+  for (let i = 0; i < retries; i++) { r = await post('/api/message', { text, model: 'm' }); if (r.status !== 409) return r; await sleep(50); }
+  return r;
+}
+// the event loop: how late a 10 ms timer fires while something else runs (a sync stall shows here)
+function loopLag() { let worst = 0, last = Date.now(); const iv = setInterval(() => { const n = Date.now(); worst = Math.max(worst, n - last - 10); last = n; }, 10); return () => { clearInterval(iv); return worst; }; }
+

+  if (ONLY.has('1')) {

+        if (r.status === 0 && /^20 MB/.test(label) && /ECONNRESET|UND_ERR_SOCKET|EPIPE/.test(r.err)) capReset.push(p);   // (readBody's documented cap: the socket destroyed)
+        else if (r.status === 0) bad.push(`POST ${p} [${label}] -> no answer (${r.err}, ${r.ms} ms)`);
+        else if (r.status >= 500) bad.push(`POST ${p} [${label}] -> ${r.status} ${r.text.slice(0, 140)}`);
+        else if (STACK_RE.test(r.text)) bad.push(`POST ${p} [${label}] -> ${r.status} with a stack trace: ${r.text.slice(0, 160)}`);

+    if (slow.length) note(`slow answers (>5 s): ${slow.join('; ')}`);
+    if (capReset.length) note(`a 20 MB body over the route's own readBody cap: the socket reset, no status (documented in readBody; the client cannot tell why): ${[...new Set(capReset)].join(', ')}`);
+    for (const b of bad) console.log('    ' + b);
+    ok(!bad.some((b) => /update-channel \[null\]/.test(b)), '[PRODUCT] POST /api/update-channel with the JSON body null: not a 500 (b.beta read on null)');
+    ok(!bad.filter((b) => !/update-channel \[null\]/.test(b)).length, `every malformed POST: an answer, not 5xx, no stack (${bad.length} bad)`);

+  }
+
+  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  if (ONLY.has('3')) {
+    console.log('\n[3] the message flow under stress');
+    await ensureProject();
+    await waitIdle();
+    await post('/api/clear-conversation', {});
+
+    // (a) 30 messages back-to-back while a run is going: /api/message 409s each; /api/steer queues them
+    // (marker words lower-case: an upper-case word like MARK is masked to a token on its way to the model)
+    A.queue = [B_.text('the base reply, slowly. '.repeat(10), 8, 40)];   // ~30 chunks x 40 ms
+    A.record = true; A.seen = []; A.firstChunkAt = 0;
+    const r0 = await send('base task');
+    ok(r0.status === 200, `a slow run starts (${r0.status})`);
+    await until(async () => A.firstChunkAt, 5000);
+    const msgs = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => post('/api/message', { text: 'burst ' + i })));
+    const codes = msgs.map((r) => r.status);
+    ok(codes.every((c) => c === 409), `30 messages during a run: each 409 'already running' (${[...new Set(codes)].join(',')})`);
+    const steers = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) steers.push(await post('/api/steer', { text: `steer ${i} please` }));
+    const acked = steers.map((r, i) => (r.status === 200 && r.json && r.json.ok ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0);
+    const s1 = await waitIdle(20000);
+    ok(!!s1 && s1.status === 'done', `the steered run ends done (${s1 && s1.status})`);
+    const seenAll = A.seen.join('\n');
+    const lost = acked.filter((i) => !new RegExp(`steer ${i} please`).test(seenAll));
+    note(`model calls seen during the steered run: ${A.seen.length}; the last one ends ${JSON.stringify(String(A.seen[A.seen.length - 1] || '').slice(-400))}`);
+    note(`/api/steer x30: ${acked.length} answered ok, queued counts ${steers.map((r) => r.json && r.json.queued).join(',')}; statuses ${[...new Set(steers.map((r) => r.status))].join(',')}`);
+    ok(lost.length === 0, `[PRODUCT] every steer the server answered ok:true reaches the model (${lost.length} of ${acked.length} lost: ${lost.slice(0, 5).map((i) => 'steer ' + i).join(', ')}${lost.length > 5 ? ' ...' : ''})`);
+    A.record = false; A.seen = [];
+    await waitIdle();
+
+    // (b) cancel mid-stream, 20 times
+    let cancelOk = 0; const cancelMs = []; const cancelBad = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
+      A.queue = [B_.text('streaming that will be cancelled '.repeat(20), 10, 30)];
+      A.firstChunkAt = 0;
+      const r = await send('cancel me ' + i);
+      if (r.status !== 200) { cancelBad.push(`#${i} send ${r.status} ${r.text.slice(0, 60)}`); continue; }
+      await until(async () => A.firstChunkAt, 5000, 5);
+      await sleep(i % 3 * 40);
+      const t0 = Date.now();
+      const c = await post('/api/cancel', {});
+      const s = await until(async () => { const x = (await get('/api/state?limit=1')).json; return x && x.status !== 'running' ? x : null; }, 5000, 10);
+      cancelMs.push(Date.now() - t0);
+      if (c.status === 200 && s && s.status === 'idle') cancelOk++; else cancelBad.push(`#${i} cancel ${c.status} status ${s && s.status}`);
+    }
+    await sleep(300);
+    const sc = await st();
+    const ce = evs(sc);
+    ok(cancelOk === 20, `cancel mid-stream x20: idle each time (${cancelOk}/20; ${cancelBad.slice(0, 3).join('; ')})`);
+    ok(Math.max(...cancelMs) < 2000, `each cancel is prompt (max ${Math.max(...cancelMs)} ms, mean ${Math.round(cancelMs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / cancelMs.length)} ms)`);
+    // each cancelled message either taken back (gone) or kept with a run-interrupted after it - never a reply after the marker
+    const userIdx = ce.map((e, i) => (e.type === 'user-message' && /^cancel me \d+$/.test(e.content || '') ? i : -1)).filter((i) => i >= 0);
+    const unmarked = userIdx.filter((ui) => { const nextUser = userIdx.find((j) => j > ui) ?? ce.length; return !ce.slice(ui + 1, nextUser).some((e) => e.type === 'run-interrupted'); });
+    ok(unmarked.length === 0, `every cancelled message kept in the chat has its interruption marker (${userIdx.length} kept, ${unmarked.length} without)`);
+    const afterMarker = ce.filter((e, i) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && /streaming that will be cancelled/.test(e.content || '') && ce.slice(0, i).some((x) => x.type === 'run-interrupted') && i > ce.map((x) => x.type).lastIndexOf('run-interrupted'));
+    ok(afterMarker.length === 0, `no cancelled reply lands after the last interruption marker (${afterMarker.length})`);
+    timings.cancelMsMax = Math.max(...cancelMs);
+
+    // (c) 20 cancels at once on one run: one marker
+    A.queue = [B_.text('one run, many cancels '.repeat(30), 10, 30)];
+    A.firstChunkAt = 0;
+    await send('many cancels');
+    await until(async () => A.firstChunkAt, 5000, 5);
+    await sleep(150);
+    const before = evs(await st()).filter((e) => e.type === 'run-interrupted').length;
+    const cs = await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: 20 }, () => post('/api/cancel', {})));
+    await waitIdle();
+    await sleep(300);
+    const after = evs(await st()).filter((e) => e.type === 'run-interrupted').length;
+    ok(cs.every((r) => r.status === 200), `20 concurrent cancels: each 200 (${[...new Set(cs.map((r) => r.status))].join(',')})`);
+    ok(after - before <= 1, `20 concurrent cancels on one run: at most one interruption marker (${after - before})`);
+
+    // (d) clear-conversation during a run: refused, the run unharmed
+    A.queue = [B_.text('a reply that clear must not cut. '.repeat(10), 10, 30)];
+    await send('clear during me');
+    await until(async () => A.firstChunkAt, 5000, 5);
+    const clr = await post('/api/clear-conversation', {});
+    const reset = await post('/api/reset', {});
+    const rp = await post('/api/reset-project', {});
+    ok(clr.status === 409 && reset.status === 409 && rp.status === 409, `clear / reset / reset-project during a run: 409 (${clr.status}, ${reset.status}, ${rp.status})`);
+    const sd = await waitIdle(15000);
+    ok(sd && sd.status === 'done', `the run goes on to done (${sd && sd.status})`);
+    const lastReply = evs(await st()).filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').pop();
+    ok(lastReply && /a reply that clear must not cut/.test(lastReply.content || ''), 'its reply is whole in the chat');
+    const clr2 = await post('/api/clear-conversation', {});
+    const afterClr = await st();
+    ok(clr2.status === 200 && evs(afterClr).filter((e) => e.type === 'user-message').length === 0, `clear after the run: 200, no messages shown (${clr2.status}, ${evs(afterClr).length} events)`);
+
+    // (e) SSE: 2 clients stay; 10 more connect and drop, 5 rounds; a stalled reader
+    const sse = (label) => {
+      const c = { label, frames: [], buf: '', ac: new AbortController(), done: null };
+      c.done = fetch(B + '/api/stream', { signal: c.ac.signal }).then(async (r) => {
+        const rd = r.body.getReader(); const dec = new TextDecoder();
+        for (;;) { const { value, done } = await rd.read(); if (done) break; c.buf += dec.decode(value, { stream: true }); let k; while ((k = c.buf.indexOf('\n\n')) >= 0) { const f = c.buf.slice(0, k); c.buf = c.buf.slice(k + 2); const d = f.split('\n').filter((l) => l.startsWith('data: ')).map((l) => l.slice(6)).join('\n'); if (d) { try { c.frames.push(JSON.parse(d)); } catch { c.frames.push({ raw: d }); } } } }
+      }).catch(() => {});
+      return c;
+    };
+    const s1c = sse('one'), s2c = sse('two');
+    await until(async () => s1c.frames.length && s2c.frames.length, 5000);
+    ok(s1c.frames[0] && s1c.frames[0].type === 'init' && s2c.frames[0] && s2c.frames[0].type === 'init', 'two SSE clients get their init');
+    const w0 = (await get('/api/health')).json.windows;
+    const churnT0 = Date.now();
+    for (let round = 0; round < 5; round++) {
+      const extra = Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => sse('x' + round + '-' + i));
+      await sleep(round % 2 ? 5 : 60);   // some drop before their init, some after
+      for (const c of extra) c.ac.abort();
+      await Promise.all(extra.map((c) => c.done));
+    }
+    // raw sockets destroyed mid-headers, and mid-stream without a FIN
+    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) { await new Promise((res) => { const rq = http.get(`${B}/api/stream`, (rs) => { rs.once('data', () => { rq.destroy(); res(); }); }); rq.on('error', res); setTimeout(res, 2000); }); }
+    timings.sseChurnMs = Date.now() - churnT0;
+    await sleep(500);
+    const w1 = (await get('/api/health')).json.windows;
+    ok(w1 === w0, `after 60 SSE clients came and went, the window count is back where it was (${w0} -> ${w1})`);
+    // a reader that stalls: the socket paused, never read
+    let stalled = null;
+    await new Promise((res) => { const rq = http.get(`${B}/api/stream`, (rs) => { rs.pause(); stalled = { rq, rs }; res(); }); rq.on('error', res); });
+    const n1 = s1c.frames.length, n2 = s2c.frames.length;
+    A.queue = [B_.text('frames for both clients. '.repeat(40), 20, 5)];
+    await send('sse run');
+    const sseDone = await waitIdle(20000);
+    await sleep(500);
+    const got1 = s1c.frames.slice(n1), got2 = s2c.frames.slice(n2);
+    const sawDone = (fs) => fs.some((f) => (f.type === 'status' && f.status === 'done') || JSON.stringify(f).includes('"status":"done"'));
+    ok(sseDone && sseDone.status === 'done', `a run with a stalled SSE reader connected ends (${sseDone && sseDone.status})`);
+    ok(got1.length > 0 && got2.length > 0 && sawDone(got1) && sawDone(got2), `both lasting SSE clients get the run's frames and its done (${got1.length}, ${got2.length})`);
+    ok(await health(), '/api/health answers after the SSE churn');
+
+    // (f) a 2 MB reply in 1-byte chunks (the stalled reader still connected)  (CB_EDGE_SKIP_2MB=1 leaves it out)
+    // The mock gives the event loop a turn every 256 chunks, as network delivery would. Contract asked: the run ends
+    // (done, the reply whole) in a bounded time - 180 s here - with /api/health answering throughout. Past the bound the
+    // run is cancelled: the cancel must still work in the middle of the flood.
+    if (process.env.CB_EDGE_SKIP_2MB !== '1') {
+    const N2 = 2 * 1024 * 1024, LIMIT2 = Number(process.env.CB_EDGE_2MB_LIMIT_MS) || 180000;
+    A.queue = [B_.bytes(N2)]; A.bytesOut = 0;
+    const heap0 = process.memoryUsage();
+    const lag2 = loopLag();
+    const t2 = Date.now();
+    const r2 = await send('give me 2 MB');
+    let healthWorst = 0; let healthFails = 0; let stop2 = false;
+    const prober = (async () => { while (!stop2) { const h = await get('/api/health', { timeout: 10000 }); healthWorst = Math.max(healthWorst, h.ms); if (h.status !== 200) healthFails++; await sleep(250); } })();
+    const s2 = await until(async () => { const x = (await get('/api/state?limit=1', { timeout: 30000 })).json; return x && x.status !== 'running' ? x : null; }, LIMIT2, 500);
+    const elapsed2 = Date.now() - t2, bytesBy = A.bytesOut;
+    timings.reply2MB1ByteMs = elapsed2; timings.reply2MBBytesDelivered = bytesBy; timings.reply2MBChunksPerSec = Math.round(bytesBy / (elapsed2 / 1000));
+    timings.reply2MBProjectedMs = bytesBy ? Math.round(elapsed2 * N2 / bytesBy) : null;
+    let cancelled2 = null;
+    if (!s2) {
+      const tc = Date.now(); const c = await post('/api/cancel', {}, { timeout: 60000 });
+      const sc2 = await until(async () => { const x = (await get('/api/state?limit=1', { timeout: 30000 })).json; return x && x.status !== 'running' ? x : null; }, 60000, 200);
+      cancelled2 = { status: c.status, ms: Date.now() - tc, after: sc2 && sc2.status };
+      await until(async () => A.inflight === 0, 30000, 100);
+    }
+    stop2 = true; await prober;
+    timings.reply2MBLoopStall = lag2(); timings.reply2MBHealthWorstMs = healthWorst;
+    const heap1 = process.memoryUsage();
+    timings.reply2MBHeapDeltaMB = Math.round((heap1.heapUsed - heap0.heapUsed) / 1048576); timings.reply2MBRssMB = Math.round(heap1.rss / 1048576);
+    ok(r2.status === 200 && s2 && s2.status === 'done', `[PRODUCT perf: per-chunk cost] a 2 MB reply in 1-byte chunks ends done within ${LIMIT2 / 1000} s (${s2 ? s2.status + ' in ' + elapsed2 + ' ms' : `still running: ${bytesBy} of ${N2} bytes taken in ${elapsed2} ms, ~${timings.reply2MBChunksPerSec} chunks/s, projected ${Math.round((timings.reply2MBProjectedMs || 0) / 1000)} s`})`);
+    if (s2) {
+      const big = evs(await st()).filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text').pop();
+      const bigLen = big ? String(big.content || '').length : 0;
+      ok(bigLen >= N2 - 200, `the 2 MB reply is in the chat whole (${bigLen} of ${N2} chars)`);
+      const sz = await get('/api/state?limit=1000000', { timeout: 30000 });
+      note(`/api/state with the 2 MB reply in it: ${sz.text.length} bytes in ${sz.ms} ms`);
+    } else {
+      ok(cancelled2 && cancelled2.status === 200 && cancelled2.after === 'idle' && cancelled2.ms < 5000, `a cancel in the middle of the flood works (${JSON.stringify(cancelled2)})`);
+    }
+    ok(healthWorst < 2000 && healthFails === 0, `/api/health stays answering during the 2 MB stream (worst ${healthWorst} ms, ${healthFails} failed; event-loop stall ${timings.reply2MBLoopStall} ms)`);
+    }
+    try { stalled && stalled.rq.destroy(); } catch {}
+    await sleep(300);
+    s1c.ac.abort(); s2c.ac.abort();
+    await post('/api/clear-conversation', {});
+
+    // (g) a reply with no chunks at all
+    A.queue = [B_.empty(), B_.empty(), B_.empty(), B_.empty(), B_.empty()];
+    const callsE = A.calls;
+    const te = Date.now();
+    const re = await send('nothing comes back');
+    const se = await waitIdle(60000);
+    timings.emptyReplyMs = Date.now() - te;
+    note(`0-chunk reply: ended ${se && se.status} after ${timings.emptyReplyMs} ms and ${A.calls - callsE} model call(s); error=${JSON.stringify(se && se.error)}; last events ${evs(await st()).slice(-3).map((e) => e.type).join(',')}`);
+    ok(re.status === 200 && se && se.status !== 'running', `a 0-chunk reply: the run ends (${se && se.status}, ${timings.emptyReplyMs} ms)`);
+    A.queue = [];
+    const rAfterE = await send('after the empty reply'); const sAfterE = await waitIdle();
+    ok(rAfterE.status === 200 && sAfterE && sAfterE.status === 'done', `the next message after it runs to done (${sAfterE && sAfterE.status})`);
+
+    // (h) an adapter that throws mid-stream
+    A.queue = Array.from({ length: 8 }, () => B_.throwMid());
+    const callsT = A.calls; const tt = Date.now();
+    const rt = await send('throw please');
+    const stt = await waitIdle(120000);
+    timings.throwMidMs = Date.now() - tt;
+    note(`throw mid-stream: ${stt && stt.status} after ${timings.throwMidMs} ms, ${A.calls - callsT} call(s), error=${JSON.stringify(stt && stt.error).slice(0, 120)}`);
+    ok(rt.status === 200 && stt && stt.status !== 'running', `a throw mid-stream: the run ends (${stt && stt.status})`);
+    ok(stt && (stt.status === 'error' || stt.status === 'done') && !STACK_RE.test(JSON.stringify(stt.error || '')), `... as an error with a message, no stack (${stt && stt.status}: ${String(stt && stt.error).slice(0, 80)})`);
+    A.queue = [];
+    const rAfterT = await send('after the throw'); const sAfterT = await waitIdle();
+    ok(rAfterT.status === 200 && sAfterT && sAfterT.status === 'done', `the next message after the throw runs to done (${sAfterT && sAfterT.status})`);
+
+    // (i) an adapter that hangs, honouring the abort signal
+    A.queue = [B_.hangHonor()];
+    await send('hang (honours abort)');
+    await until(async () => A.inflight > 0, 5000, 10);
+    await sleep(300);
+    const th = Date.now(); const ch = await post('/api/cancel', {});
+    const sh = await until(async () => { const x = (await get('/api/state?limit=1')).json; return x && x.status !== 'running' ? x : null; }, 5000, 10);
+    ok(ch.status === 200 && sh && sh.status === 'idle', `cancel a hung (abort-honouring) adapter: idle in ${Date.now() - th} ms (${sh && sh.status})`);
+    await sleep(500);
+    ok(A.inflight === 0, `the hung call was let go (in flight: ${A.inflight})`);
+    const rAfterH = await send('after the hang'); const sAfterH = await waitIdle();
+    ok(rAfterH.status === 200 && sAfterH && sAfterH.status === 'done', `the next message runs to done (${rAfterH.status} ${sAfterH && sAfterH.status})`);
+
+    // (j) an adapter that hangs and ignores the signal (a dead socket the adapter never notices)
+    A.queue = [B_.hangIgnore()];
+    await send('hang (ignores abort)');
+    await until(async () => A.hungForever > 0, 5000, 10);
+    await sleep(300);
+    const tj = Date.now(); const cj = await post('/api/cancel', {});
+    const sj = await until(async () => { const x = (await get('/api/state?limit=1')).json; return x && x.status !== 'running' ? x : null; }, 5000, 10);
+    ok(cj.status === 200 && sj && sj.status === 'idle', `cancel a hung adapter that ignores the signal: idle in ${Date.now() - tj} ms (${sj && sj.status})`);
+    const rAfterJ = await send('after the deaf hang');
+    const sAfterJ = await waitIdle(15000);
+    ok(rAfterJ.status === 200 && sAfterJ && sAfterJ.status === 'done', `the next message after it is accepted and runs to done (${rAfterJ.status} ${rAfterJ.text.slice(0, 60)}; ${sAfterJ && sAfterJ.status})`);
+    const ej = evs(await st());
+    const ackAfter = ej.findIndex((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && e.content === 'after the deaf hang');
+    ok(ackAfter >= 0 && ej.slice(ackAfter).some((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text'), 'its reply is in the chat after it');
+    // and again: a second run after the orphaned one, then a cancel of a normal slow run still works
+    A.queue = [B_.text('slow after deaf '.repeat(30), 10, 30)]; A.firstChunkAt = 0;
+    await send('slow after deaf'); await until(async () => A.firstChunkAt, 5000, 5);
+    const cj2 = await post('/api/cancel', {}); const sj2 = await waitIdle(5000);
+    ok(cj2.status === 200 && sj2 && sj2.status === 'idle', `with the deaf call still hanging, a later run still cancels (${sj2 && sj2.status})`);
+    const act = (await get('/api/llm-activity')).json;
+    const openRows = act && Array.isArray(act.calls) ? act.calls.filter((c) => c.endedAt == null).length : -1;
+    note(`/api/llm-activity rows still "running" (the deaf hang's among them): ${openRows}`);
+    ok(await health(), '/api/health answers after the stress cases');
+  }
+
+  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  if (ONLY.has('4')) {
+    console.log('\n[4] VOLUME: 300 sequential messages');
+    await ensureProject();
+    await waitIdle();
+    A.queue = []; A.record = false;
+    await post('/api/clear-conversation', {});
+    let k = 0;
+    A.defaultReply = () => `vol reply ${++k}.`;
+    const N = 300, lat = [], m0 = process.memoryUsage(), T0 = Date.now(), errors = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
+      const t0 = Date.now();
+      const r = await send(`vol-${i}`);
+      if (r.status !== 200) { errors.push(`#${i} ${r.status} ${r.text.slice(0, 60)}`); continue; }
+      const s = await until(async () => { const x = (await get('/api/state?limit=1')).json; return x && x.status !== 'running' ? x : null; }, 20000, 5);
+      if (!s || s.status !== 'done') errors.push(`#${i} ended ${s && s.status}`);
+      lat.push(Date.now() - t0);
+    }
+    const total = Date.now() - T0, m1 = process.memoryUsage();
+    A.defaultReply = () => 'ack.';
+    const sv = await st();
+    const ve = evs(sv);
+    const um = ve.filter((e) => e.type === 'user-message' && /^vol-\d+$/.test(e.content || ''));
+    const at = ve.filter((e) => e.type === 'assistant-text' && /^vol reply \d+\.$/.test(String(e.content || '').trim()));
+    const sorted = [...lat].sort((a, b) => a - b), pct = (q) => sorted[Math.min(sorted.length - 1, Math.floor(q * sorted.length))];
+    const avg = (a) => Math.round(a.reduce((x, y) => x + y, 0) / Math.max(1, a.length));
+    timings.volume = { total, p50: pct(0.5), p95: pct(0.95), max: sorted[sorted.length - 1], first30avg: avg(lat.slice(0, 30)), last30avg: avg(lat.slice(-30)), heapDeltaMB: Math.round((m1.heapUsed - m0.heapUsed) / 1048576), rssMB: Math.round(m1.rss / 1048576), modelCalls: k };
+    const ss = await get('/api/state?limit=1000000'); timings.volume.stateBytes = ss.text.length; timings.volume.stateMs = ss.ms;
+    console.log('  timings', JSON.stringify(timings.volume));
+    ok(errors.length === 0, `300 messages: each accepted and done (${errors.length} not: ${errors.slice(0, 3).join('; ')})`);
+    ok(sv.status === 'done' || sv.status === 'idle', `status at the end: ${sv.status}`);
+    ok(um.length === N, `one user-message per message (${um.length}/${N})`);
+    ok(at.length === N, `one reply per message (${at.length}/${N}; ${k} model calls)`);
+    ok(new Set(um.map((e) => e.id)).size === um.length && new Set(ve.map((e) => e.id)).size === ve.length, 'event ids unique');
+    const order = um.map((e) => Number(e.content.slice(4)));
+    ok(order.every((v, i) => v === i), 'the messages in the order sent');
+    ok(timings.volume.last30avg < Math.max(200, timings.volume.first30avg * 5), `no runaway slow-down: last 30 avg ${timings.volume.last30avg} ms vs first 30 avg ${timings.volume.first30avg} ms`);
+    ok(await health(), '/api/health answers after the volume run');
+  }
+} catch (e) {
+  fail++; failures.push('harness error: ' + (e && e.stack || e));
+  console.log('  FAIL harness error:', e && e.stack || e);
+} finally {
+  console.log('\ntimings ' + JSON.stringify(timings));
+  if (failures.length) { console.log('\nfailures:'); for (const f of failures) console.log('  - ' + f.slice(0, 300)); }
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  clearTimeout(WATCHDOG);
+  try { server.closeAllConnections?.(); } catch {}
+  try { server.close(); } catch {}
+  // (the junction, if still there, first: rmdir takes the link only, never what it points at)
+  try { if (existsSync(path.join(PROJ, 'lnk', 'outside.txt'))) execSync(`rmdir "${path.join(PROJ, 'lnk')}"`, { shell: 'cmd.exe', stdio: 'ignore' }); } catch {}

+/**
+ * THE MANDATORY FILTER'S GAP (found live, 2026-11-18): piishape was reloaded while a snippet read a file part by part.
+ * The parts read after the reload came back unmasked, and tokens issued before it were written to a file as they
+ * were - only piishape turns its own tokens back. Sends to the model were already refused without it; now a tool's
+ * result (mask) and a tool's arguments (unmask) are refused too, while a mandatory filter that WAS in is not.
+ *   [1] in: a name is masked, its token turns back
+ *   [2] its file gone (a reload, a replacement): the result is refused, not masked by less; an unmask is refused, not
+ *       written with the token in; the refusal says why and what to do
+ *   [3] back: masking and unmasking work again, the earlier token turns back
+ *   [4] never in (a test, a build without it): nothing stands in - unchanged
+ *   [5] CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS=0: nothing stands in
+ * Run: node test/test-mandatory-reload-gap.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtemp, mkdir, writeFile, readFile, rm } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+const home = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-reloadgap-'));
+process.env.CB_HOME = home;
+delete process.env.CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS;
+const { createPluginManager } = await import('../src/plugins/index.mjs');
+const { createFilterPipeline } = await import('../src/plugins/filters.mjs');
+const { createTokenStore } = await import('../src/tokens.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const refusal = async (p) => { try { await p; return null; } catch (e) { return String((e && e.message) || e); } };
+
+const dir = path.join(home, '.code_boss', 'plugins');
+await mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
+const file = path.join(dir, 'piishape__1.mjs');
+const source = await readFile(path.join(ROOT, 'examples', 'plugins', 'piishape__1.mjs'), 'utf8');
+await writeFile(file, source);
+const store = createTokenStore({ secret: 'k' });
+const pm = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, pluginDataRoot: path.join(home, 'data'), tokens: store });
+pm.setPluginsDir(dir);
+await pm.reconcile();
+const pipe = createFilterPipeline(() => pm.enabledFilters(), { ownNames: () => pm.exemptNames(), namesVersion: () => pm.namesVersion(), tokens: store });
+const proj = path.join(home, 'proj'); await mkdir(proj, { recursive: true });
+const ctx = { hook: 'tool-result', toolName: 'js_eval', projectDir: proj };
+const TEXT = 'id,first_name,last_name,city\n1,Zoltan,Qorvathy,Denver\n';
+
+try {
+  console.log('\n[1] in');
+  const masked = await pipe.mask(TEXT, ctx);
+  ok(!/Qorvathy/.test(masked), `the name is masked (${masked.replace(/\n/g, ' | ').slice(0, 90)})`);
+  const tok = (masked.match(/#X[x]+#[0-9a-z]{7}/) || [])[0];
+  ok(!!tok && (await pipe.unmask(tok, { hook: 'tool-args', toolName: 'write' })) !== tok, `its token turns back (${tok})`);
+
+  console.log('\n[2] its file gone: a reload, a replacement');
+  await rm(file);
+  await pm.reconcile();
+  ok(!pm.enabledFilters().some((f) => f.plugin === 'piishape' && !f.standIn), 'piishape is out');
+  const r1 = await refusal(pipe.mask(TEXT, ctx));
+  ok(!!r1 && /piishape is not running/.test(r1) && /Nothing was let through/.test(r1), `a result is REFUSED, not masked by less (${(r1 || 'passed: ' + 'masked').slice(0, 120)})`);
+  const r2 = await refusal(pipe.unmask(`write this: ${tok}`, { hook: 'tool-args', toolName: 'write' }));
+  ok(!!r2 && /tokens could not be turned back/.test(r2), `an unmask is refused, not written with the token in (${(r2 || '').slice(0, 100)})`);
+  ok(!!r1 && /Try the same call again in a moment/.test(r1) && /Settings › Plugins/.test(r1), 'the refusal says to try again, and where to look');
+
+  console.log('\n[3] back');
+  await writeFile(file, source);
+  await pm.reconcile();
+  const again = await pipe.mask(TEXT, ctx);
+  ok(!/Qorvathy/.test(again), 'masking works again');
+  ok((await pipe.unmask(tok, { hook: 'tool-args', toolName: 'write' })) !== tok, 'and the token issued before the gap turns back');
+
+  console.log('\n[4] never in');
+  {
+    const home2 = await mkdtemp(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-reloadgap-none-'));
+    const dir2 = path.join(home2, 'plugins'); await mkdir(dir2, { recursive: true });
+    const pm2 = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, pluginDataRoot: path.join(home2, 'data'), tokens: createTokenStore({ secret: 'k' }) });
+    pm2.setPluginsDir(dir2);
+    await pm2.reconcile();
+    ok(!pm2.enabledFilters().some((f) => f.standIn), 'a manager that never had it adds nothing (a test, a build without it - the send is refused there anyway)');
+    await rm(home2, { recursive: true, force: true });
+  }
+
+  console.log('\n[5] CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS=0');
+  await rm(file); await pm.reconcile();
+  process.env.CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS = '0';
+  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1100));   // (the switch is read at most once a second)
+  ok(!pm.enabledFilters().some((f) => f.standIn), 'switched off: nothing stands in');
+  delete process.env.CB_MANDATORY_PLUGINS;
+  await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1100));
+  ok(pm.enabledFilters().some((f) => f.standIn), 'and back on, it stands in again');
+} catch (e) {
+  fail++; console.log('  FAIL harness:', e && e.stack || e);
+} finally {
+  try { await pm.dispose?.(); } catch {}
+  try { await rm(home, { recursive: true, force: true }); } catch {}
+  console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+  process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+}
