=== add test/test-parse-edges-volume.mjs
new 1c711df7b9c2969fe6c36956
+/*
+ * EDGE CASES + VOLUME for the agent-output parsing layer:
+ *   src/agent/tag-lexer.mjs, tool-parser.mjs, turn-tags.mjs, prose-sanitizer.mjs, and the reply parser they feed
+ *   (turn-stream.mjs parseResponseSegments + the live pump in runTurnStreaming).
+ *
+ * Every expectation is taken from a contract the code states in its comments. Where the contract is unclear the
+ * behaviour is RECORDED (printed under "observed"), not asserted.
+ *
+ * Checks labelled [PRODUCT BUG] fail today on purpose: each one is a stated contract the code does not keep. They
+ * pass once the bug is fixed. Checks labelled [PERF] assert a time bound; linear code passes them by 10-100x, the
+ * quadratic regexes they target miss them by a similar margin.
+ *
+ * Run: node test/test-parse-edges-volume.mjs
+ */
+import { tokenizeTags, serializeTokens } from '../src/agent/tag-lexer.mjs';
+import { parseToolCalls, openCbTags, hasNativeToolCall, hasOrphanToolVerb } from '../src/agent/tool-parser.mjs';
+import { turnTag, TURN_TAG_RE } from '../src/agent/turn-tags.mjs';
+import {
+  sanitizeAssistantProse, truncateAtFirstBlock, holdLeadingRule, stripKeywords, createLiveProseFilter,
+  splitLeadingThought, maybeThoughtHead,
+} from '../src/agent/prose-sanitizer.mjs';
+import { parseResponseSegments, runTurnStreaming } from '../src/agent/turn-stream.mjs';
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const fails = [];
+const observed = [];
+const timings = [];
+function ok(cond, label) { if (cond) pass++; else { fail++; fails.push(label); } }
+const bug = (cond, label) => ok(cond, '[PRODUCT BUG] ' + label);
+function eq(actual, expected, label) {
+  const a = JSON.stringify(actual), b = JSON.stringify(expected);
+  ok(a === b, label + (a === b ? '' : `  (got ${a} want ${b})`));
+}
+const note = (label, value) => observed.push(`${label}: ${JSON.stringify(value)}`);
+function timed(fn) {
+  const t0 = performance.now();
+  let r, err = null;
+  try { r = fn(); } catch (e) { err = e; }
+  return { r, err, ms: performance.now() - t0 };
+}
+// assert a call finishes under a bound (and does not throw); returns its result
+function perf(label, boundMs, fn, isBug = false) {
+  const { r, err, ms } = timed(fn);
+  timings.push(`${ms.toFixed(0).padStart(6)} ms  (bound ${boundMs})  ${label}`);
+  ok(!err, `${label}: does not throw${err ? ' - THREW ' + err.message : ''}`);
+  (isBug ? bug : ok)(ms < boundMs, `[PERF] ${label}: under ${boundMs} ms (took ${ms.toFixed(0)} ms)`);
+  return r;
+}
+// seeded PRNG (mulberry32) so the fuzz is reproducible
+function rng(seed) { return () => { seed |= 0; seed = (seed + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0; let t = Math.imul(seed ^ (seed >>> 15), 1 | seed); t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t; return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296; }; }
+
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+// 1. TAG LEXER — contract clauses 1-7 (tag-lexer.mjs header)
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+function lexInvariants(input, opts, label) {
+  let toks = null, threw = null;
+  try { toks = tokenizeTags(input, opts); } catch (e) { threw = e; }
+  ok(!threw, `lexer ${label}: never throws${threw ? ' - THREW ' + threw.message : ''}`);
+  if (threw) return [];
+  const src = typeof input === 'string' ? input : '';
+  ok(serializeTokens(toks) === src, `lexer ${label}: byte-preserving`);
+  let prev = 0, good = true;
+  for (const t of toks) { if (t.start !== prev || t.raw !== src.slice(t.start, t.end)) good = false; prev = t.end; }
+  if (toks.length && prev !== src.length) good = false;
+  ok(good, `lexer ${label}: contiguous offsets, raw === slice`);
+  return toks;
+}
+const kinds = (toks) => toks.map((t) => t.type + (t.name ? ':' + t.name : '')).join(' ');
+
+{
+  // non-strings / empty / whitespace
+  for (const v of [undefined, null, 0, 42, {}, [], true, Symbol.iterator === undefined ? 1 : () => 1]) {
+    let r, threw = false; try { r = tokenizeTags(v); } catch { threw = true; }
+    ok(!threw && Array.isArray(r) && r.length === 0, `lexer: non-string ${typeof v} -> []`);
+  }
+  eq(tokenizeTags(''), [], 'lexer: empty -> []');
+  eq(kinds(lexInvariants(' \t\r\n ', undefined, 'ws-only')), 'text', 'lexer: whitespace-only is one text token');
+  eq(kinds(lexInvariants('<js>', undefined, 'only-a-tag')), 'open:js', 'lexer: a reply that is only a tag');
+  // opts that are not objects never deref-throw (clause 1)
+  for (const o of [null, 5, 'x', false]) lexInvariants('<a>', o, `opts=${JSON.stringify(o)}`);
+
+  // malformed: unclosed / close without open / nested same tags
+  eq(kinds(lexInvariants('a <b> c', undefined, 'unclosed')), 'text open:b text', 'lexer: unclosed open is still an open token (nesting is the consumer\'s job)');
+  eq(kinds(lexInvariants('a </b> c', undefined, 'stray close')), 'text close:b text', 'lexer: close without open is a close token');
+  eq(kinds(lexInvariants('<a><a>x</a></a>', undefined, 'nested same')), 'open:a open:a text close:a close:a', 'lexer: nested same tags emitted in order');
+  eq(kinds(lexInvariants('<a/>', undefined, 'self-close')), 'open:a', 'lexer: self-closing');
+  ok(tokenizeTags('<a/>')[0].selfClose === true, 'lexer: selfClose flag');
+
+  // `>` and quotes inside attribute values (clause 4 — THE bug class)
+  let t = lexInvariants('<js a=">" b=\'>"<\' c="x\\"y>">body</js>', undefined, 'gt-in-attr');
+  eq(t[0] && t[0].attrs, { a: '>', b: '>"<', c: 'x"y>' }, 'lexer: > / other quote / escaped quote inside quoted values');
+  eq(kinds(t), 'open:js text close:js', 'lexer: a quoted > never closes the tag');
+  t = lexInvariants('<x p="D:\\j\\wls\\12c" u=\'\\\\srv\\share\'>', undefined, 'backslashes');
+  eq(t[0] && t[0].attrs, { p: 'D:\\j\\wls\\12c', u: '\\\\srv\\share' }, 'lexer: lone backslashes stay literal (Windows/UNC paths)');
+  t = lexInvariants('<x a = "1"  b=2 c d=\'\'>', undefined, 'attr spacing');
+  eq(t[0] && t[0].attrs, { a: '1', b: '2', c: true, d: '' }, 'lexer: spaced =, bare value, valueless, empty value');
+
+  // case
+  t = lexInvariants('<JS Timeout_MS="5">x</JS >', undefined, 'case');
+  eq(kinds(t), 'open:JS text close:JS', 'lexer: upper-case names tokenized (name kept verbatim)');
+  ok(t[0].attrs.Timeout_MS === '5', 'lexer: attr name case kept verbatim');
+
+  // literal '<'
+  eq(kinds(lexInvariants('a < b, 3<4, <3, </ x>, <-', undefined, 'literal <')), 'text', 'lexer: < not starting a valid tag is literal text');
+  // CRLF / BOM / NUL / surrogates / emoji / RTL
+  t = lexInvariants('\uFEFF<a\r\n  b="1\r\n2"\r\n>x\r\n</a>', undefined, 'bom-crlf');
+  eq(kinds(t), 'text open:a text close:a', 'lexer: BOM is text, CRLF is whitespace inside a tag');
+  ok(t[1] && t[1].attrs.b === '1\r\n2', 'lexer: CRLF inside a quoted value preserved');
+  t = lexInvariants('x\0<a b="\0">\0</a>', undefined, 'nul');
+  eq(kinds(t), 'text open:a text close:a', 'lexer: NUL bytes are ordinary characters');
+  t = lexInvariants('\uD800<a v="\uDC00\uD83D\uDE00\u202Eabc">\u05E9\u05DC\u05D5\u05DD</a>\uDFFF', undefined, 'surrogates/rtl');
+  ok(t[1] && t[1].attrs.v === '\uDC00\uD83D\uDE00\u202Eabc', 'lexer: lone surrogates/emoji/RTL override preserved in a value');
+  note('lexer <a\\0b> (NUL in tag-name position)', tokenizeTags('<a\0b>').map((x) => [x.type, x.name, x.attrs]));
+  note('lexer <ä>x</ä> (non-ASCII name)', kinds(tokenizeTags('<ä>x</ä>')));
+
+  // code spans protected (clause 3)
+  t = lexInvariants('see `<remote-workspace>` and ```\n<js>x</js>\n``` and ``a `<b>` c``', undefined, 'code');
+  ok(!t.some((x) => x.type === 'open' || x.type === 'close'), 'lexer: tags inside inline/fenced/double-backtick code never emitted');
+  ok(t.filter((x) => x.type === 'code').length === 3, 'lexer: three code tokens');
+  t = lexInvariants('a ` b <js> c', undefined, 'stray backtick batch');
+  eq(kinds(t), 'text open:js text', 'lexer batch: an unclosed backtick is literal and a later tag still tokenizes');
+  t = lexInvariants('a ` b <js> c', { atEof: false }, 'stray backtick stream');
+  eq(kinds(t), 'text incomplete', 'lexer stream: an unclosed backtick holds the tail (clause 6)');
+
+  // streaming partials (clause 6)
+  for (const tail of ['<', '</', '<a', '<a ', '<a b', '<a b=', '<a b="x', '<a b="x\\"', '</a', '</a ', '<a /', '`x', '``x`']) {
+    const tt = lexInvariants('pre ' + tail, { atEof: false }, `partial ${JSON.stringify(tail)}`);
+    ok(tt.length && tt[tt.length - 1].type === 'incomplete' && tt[tt.length - 1].raw === tail, `lexer stream: partial ${JSON.stringify(tail)} is the incomplete tail`);
+    const tb = lexInvariants('pre ' + tail, undefined, `partial-batch ${JSON.stringify(tail)}`);
+    ok(!tb.some((x) => x.type === 'incomplete'), `lexer batch: partial ${JSON.stringify(tail)} resolves to text`);
+  }
+
+  // A `<` inside an open tag's attribute area: the lexer skips it as junk and keeps scanning attributes, so prose
+  // like "i <n" swallows the NEXT tag as attributes of a phantom <n ...> tag. Recorded; its effect on the live pump
+  // is asserted in section 4.
+  note('lexer "if i <n we stop.\\n<remote-workspace>" tokens', kinds(tokenizeTags('if i <n we stop.\n<remote-workspace><js>x</js></remote-workspace>')));
+}
+
+// streaming reconstruction fuzz (clause 6): commit every token before the 'incomplete', re-feed it with the next
+// chunk, and resolve what is left with atEof:true — the merged result must equal the one-shot batch tokenization.
+{
+  const norm = (toks) => {
+    const out = [];
+    for (const t of toks) {
+      if (t.type === 'text' && out.length && out[out.length - 1][0] === 'text') { out[out.length - 1][1] += t.raw; continue; }
+      out.push([t.type, t.raw, t.name || null, t.attrs ? JSON.stringify(t.attrs) : null, !!t.selfClose]);
+    }
+    return JSON.stringify(out);
+  };
+  const streamed = (full, cuts) => {
+    const out = [];
+    let pending = '';
+    let at = 0;
+    for (const c of [...cuts, full.length]) {
+      pending += full.slice(at, c); at = c;
+      const toks = tokenizeTags(pending, { atEof: false });
+      const last = toks[toks.length - 1];
+      if (last && last.type === 'incomplete') { out.push(...toks.slice(0, -1)); pending = last.raw; }
+      else { out.push(...toks); pending = ''; }
+    }
+    out.push(...tokenizeTags(pending, { atEof: true }));
+    return out;
+  };
+  const frags = ['<a>', '</a>', '<js t=">">', '<b x=\'"\'/>', '`', '``', '```', 'x', ' ', '\n', '\r\n', '<', '</', '"', "'", '\\',
+    '<remote-workspace>', '</remote-workspace>', '\uFEFF', '\0', '\uD83D\uDE00', '\u202E', '\uD800', '<a b="x', '/>', '=', '<n ', 'é'];
+  const R = rng(20261118);
+  let bad = 0, firstBad = null;
+  for (let k = 0; k < 3000; k++) {
+    let s = '';
+    const len = 1 + Math.floor(R() * 25);
+    for (let j = 0; j < len; j++) s += frags[Math.floor(R() * frags.length)];
+    const cuts = [];
+    for (let p = 1; p < s.length; p++) if (R() < 0.3) cuts.push(p);
+    if (norm(streamed(s, cuts)) !== norm(tokenizeTags(s))) { bad++; if (!firstBad) firstBad = { s, cuts }; }
+  }
+  ok(bad === 0, `lexer stream: chunked re-feed reconstructs batch tokenization (3000 fuzz cases, ${bad} differ${firstBad ? ' e.g. ' + JSON.stringify(firstBad) : ''})`);
+}
+
+// volume / perf (clause 7: linear, even on adversarial input)
+function realistic(n) {
+  let s = '', i = 0, blocks = 0, fences = 0;
+  while (s.length < n) {
+    s += `Paragraph ${i} with \`code ${i}\` and a <b>bold</b> word; a < b and c > d.\r\n\r\n\`\`\`js\nconst x${i} = a < b && c > d; // <js>\n\`\`\`\n`;
+    fences++;
+    if (i % 50 === 0) { s += `<remote-workspace><js timeout_ms="1000">return await read("f${i}.txt") // ">" is not a close</js></remote-workspace>\n`; blocks++; }
+    i++;
+  }
+  return { s, blocks, fences };
+}
+const R1 = realistic(1024 * 1024), R5 = realistic(5 * 1024 * 1024);
+{
+  let toks = perf('lexer batch 1 MB realistic', 1000, () => tokenizeTags(R1.s));
+  ok(serializeTokens(toks) === R1.s, 'lexer 1 MB: byte-preserving');
+  toks = perf('lexer batch 5 MB realistic', 2500, () => tokenizeTags(R5.s));
+  ok(serializeTokens(toks) === R5.s, 'lexer 5 MB: byte-preserving');
+  ok(toks.filter((t) => t.type === 'open' && t.name === 'remote-workspace').length === R5.blocks, 'lexer 5 MB: every block opener found');
+  ok(!toks.some((t) => t.type === 'open' && t.name === 'js' && R5.s.slice(t.start - 6, t.start) === 'd; // '), 'lexer 5 MB: a <js> inside a fence is never a tag');
+  perf('lexer stream-mode 5 MB realistic', 2500, () => tokenizeTags(R5.s, { atEof: false }));
+  toks = perf('lexer 10,000 small tags', 500, () => tokenizeTags('<a x="1">t</a>'.repeat(10000)));
+  ok(toks.length === 30000, 'lexer: 10,000 small tags -> 30,000 tokens');
+  toks = perf('lexer 100k-deep nesting', 1000, () => tokenizeTags('<a>'.repeat(100000) + '</a>'.repeat(100000)));
+  ok(toks.length === 200000, 'lexer: 100k-deep nesting -> 200,000 tokens');
+  for (const [label, s] of [
+    ['1M x "<"', '<'.repeat(1e6)],
+    ['250k x "<<<a"', '<<<a'.repeat(250000)],
+    ['125k x unterminated attr quote', '<a b="x '.repeat(125000)],
+    ['200k x "</a x"', '</a x'.repeat(200000)],
+    ['1M escaped quotes in one value', '<a b="' + '\\"'.repeat(1e6)],
+    ['333k x "<a "', '<a '.repeat(333000)],
+    ['1500 increasing unclosed backtick runs', (() => { let b = ''; for (let j = 1; j <= 1500; j++) b += '`'.repeat(j) + 'x'; return b; })()],
+  ]) {
+    for (const atEof of [true, false]) {
+      const r = perf(`lexer ${atEof ? 'batch' : 'stream'} pathological ${label}`, 3000, () => tokenizeTags(s, { atEof }));
+      ok(Array.isArray(r) && serializeTokens(r) === s, `lexer pathological ${label} (${atEof ? 'batch' : 'stream'}): byte-preserving`);
+    }
+  }
+}
+
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+// 2. TOOL PARSER — parseToolCalls contract (tool-parser.mjs header + JSDoc)
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+const W = (body) => `<remote-workspace>${body}</remote-workspace>`;
+const codes = (calls) => calls.map((c) => c.args.code);
+{
+  for (const v of [undefined, null, 0, {}, '', ' ']) {
+    let r, threw = false; try { r = parseToolCalls(v); } catch { threw = true; }
+    ok(!threw && Array.isArray(r) && (v === ' ' ? r.length === 0 : r.length === 0), `parseToolCalls(${JSON.stringify(v)}) -> []`);
+  }
+  let c = parseToolCalls(W('<js timeout_ms=\'900000\'>a</js>'));
+  ok(c.length === 1 && c[0].args.timeout_ms === 900000, 'parseToolCalls: single-quoted timeout_ms read');
+  c = parseToolCalls(W('<js note="a > b" timeout_ms="5">x</js>'));
+  ok(c.length === 1 && c[0].args.code === 'x' && c[0].args.note === 'a > b' && c[0].args.timeout_ms === 5, 'parseToolCalls: > inside a quoted attr does not close the open tag');
+  c = parseToolCalls(W('<js timeout_ms="soon">x</js>'));
+  ok(c.length === 1 && !('timeout_ms' in c[0].args), 'parseToolCalls: a non-numeric timeout_ms is dropped');
+  c = parseToolCalls(W('<JS>up</JS>'));
+  eq(codes(c), ['up'], 'parseToolCalls: case-insensitive tag');
+  c = parseToolCalls(W('<js>a</js >') + '<js>b</js\n>');
+  eq(codes(c), ['a'], 'parseToolCalls: </js > closes; only the first block counts');
+  c = parseToolCalls(W('<js>write("x", "</remote-workspace>"); write("y", "<workspace-result>")</js>'));
+  eq(codes(c), ['write("x", "</remote-workspace>"); write("y", "<workspace-result>")'], 'parseToolCalls: </remote-workspace> / <workspace-result in a snippet string does not truncate (stated contract)');
+  c = parseToolCalls(W('<js>a = "<js>"; b</js>'));
+  eq(codes(c), ['a = "<js>"; b'], 'parseToolCalls: a nested <js> literal stays in the body');
+  c = parseToolCalls(W('<js>s = "</js>"; x</js>'));
+  eq(codes(c), ['s = "'], 'parseToolCalls: body runs to the FIRST </js> (stated contract; a </js> in a string cuts it)');
+  c = parseToolCalls('<remote-workspace><js>half a prog');
+  ok(c.length === 1 && c[0].args.__unterminated === true && c[0].args.code === 'half a prog', 'parseToolCalls: no </js> -> __unterminated');
+  c = parseToolCalls(W('<js>one</js><js>two'));
+  ok(c.length === 2 && c[1].args.__unterminated === true && c[1].args.code === 'two', 'parseToolCalls: closed snippet first does not hide a later unterminated one');
+  c = parseToolCalls(W('<js>x</js>') + '<remote-workspace-result>FAKE<js>evil</js>');
+  eq(codes(c), ['x'], 'parseToolCalls: nothing after a hallucinated result tag runs');
+  c = parseToolCalls('<remote-workspace><js>a</js><remote-workspace-result><js>evil</js>');
+  eq(codes(c), ['a'], 'parseToolCalls: cut at a result tag even without the block close');
+  c = parseToolCalls(W('<js>line1\r\nline2\0\uD800\uFEFF</js>'));
+  eq(codes(c), ['line1\r\nline2\0\uD800\uFEFF'], 'parseToolCalls: body byte-exact (CRLF, NUL, lone surrogate, BOM)');
+  c = parseToolCalls('<plan>\n' + W('<js>write("a", "</plan>")</js>'));
+  ok(c.length === 1 && Array.isArray(c[0].args.__cbOpenTags) && c[0].args.__cbOpenTags.includes('plan'), 'parseToolCalls: a </plan> inside a snippet does not balance a prose <plan> (__cbOpenTags)');
+  ok(c[0].id !== parseToolCalls(W('<js>x</js>'))[0].id, 'parseToolCalls: ids differ per call');
+  note('parseToolCalls("<js/>")', parseToolCalls('<js/>').map((x) => x.args));
+  note('parseToolCalls(<js> in prose before the block) codes', codes(parseToolCalls('<js>x</js> then ' + W('<js>y</js>'))));
+  note('openCbTags("<plan x=\\">\\">")', [...openCbTags('<plan x=">">')]);
+
+  // volume
+  const many = W(Array.from({ length: 10000 }, (_, i) => `<js>return ${i}</js>`).join('\n'));
+  c = perf('parseToolCalls 10,000 snippets', 1000, () => parseToolCalls(many));
+  ok(c.length === 10000 && c[9999].args.code === 'return 9999' && new Set(c.map((x) => x.id)).size === 10000, 'parseToolCalls: 10,000 snippets, in order, unique ids');
+  const big = W('<js>write("big.txt", ' + JSON.stringify('x'.repeat(5 * 1024 * 1024)) + ')</js>');
+  c = perf('parseToolCalls one 5 MB snippet', 1000, () => parseToolCalls(big));
+  ok(c.length === 1 && c[0].args.code.length === 5 * 1024 * 1024 + 20, 'parseToolCalls: 5 MB body intact');
+  perf('parseToolCalls 5 MB realistic reply', 2000, () => parseToolCalls(R5.s));
+  // pathological: many openers that never close. The open-tag regex /<js\b((?:[^>"']|"[^"]*"|'[^']*')*?)>([\s\S]*?)<\/js\s*>/
+  // retries from every <js, each attempt scanning to the end -> O(n^2).
+  perf('parseToolCalls 20,000 unclosed "<js>" (80 KB)', 150, () => parseToolCalls('<js>'.repeat(20000)), true);
+  perf('parseToolCalls 2,000 "<js a=\\"x " unterminated quotes (18 KB)', 150, () => parseToolCalls('<js a="x '.repeat(2000)), true);
+  perf('hasOrphanToolVerb 6,000 "<js " with no ">" (24 KB)', 150, () => hasOrphanToolVerb('<js '.repeat(6000)), true);
+  perf('openCbTags 6,000 "<plan " with no ">" (36 KB)', 150, () => openCbTags('<plan '.repeat(6000)), true);
+  ok(hasNativeToolCall('<function_calls><invoke name="x">') && hasNativeToolCall('<tool_call>') && !hasNativeToolCall('<js>'), 'hasNativeToolCall: native shapes only');
+}
+
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+// 3. REPLY SEGMENTATION — parseResponseSegments (the execution path: minimal-worker runs THESE blocks)
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+const blocksOf = (segs) => segs.filter((s) => s.type === 'tool-block');
+const proseOf = (segs) => segs.filter((s) => s.type === 'prose').map((s) => s.content).join('');
+{
+  eq(parseResponseSegments(''), [], 'segments: empty -> []');
+  eq(parseResponseSegments(null), [], 'segments: non-string -> []');
+  let s = parseResponseSegments('Example: `<remote-workspace><js>rm()</js></remote-workspace>` and\n```\n<remote-workspace><js>rm()</js></remote-workspace>\n```\ndone');
+  ok(blocksOf(s).length === 0, 'segments: a block SHOWN in inline code or a fence is never executed (stated: matches the display lexer)');
+  s = parseResponseSegments('\uFEFFHi\r\n<REMOTE-WORKSPACE><js>a</js></Remote-Workspace>\r\nafter');
+  ok(blocksOf(s).length === 1 && blocksOf(s)[0].calls[0].args.code === 'a' && proseOf(s).includes('after'), 'segments: BOM, CRLF, mixed-case envelope');
+  s = parseResponseSegments('a <remote-workspace><js>x</js></remote-workspace> b <remote-workspace><js>y</js></remote-workspace> c');
+  ok(blocksOf(s).length === 2 && proseOf(s) === 'a  b  c', 'segments: two blocks, prose around/between kept');
+  s = parseResponseSegments('<remote-workspace><js>x</js>');
+  ok(blocksOf(s).length === 0, 'segments: an unclosed block is prose (never run partial)');
+  s = parseResponseSegments('<remote-workspace><remote-workspace><js>x</js></remote-workspace></remote-workspace>');
+  note('segments: nested envelope', s.map((x) => x.type + (x.calls ? ':' + x.calls.length : '')));
+  s = parseResponseSegments('i <n is the loop bound.\n<remote-workspace><js>x</js></remote-workspace>');
+  ok(blocksOf(s).length === 1, 'segments: "i <n" in prose does not hide the block (regex path)');
+
+  // tool-parser.mjs states "a snippet containing `</remote-workspace>` or `<workspace-result` in a string cannot
+  // truncate the block" - true of parseToolCalls alone, but parseResponseSegments finds the block's close / the
+  // result cut on the raw text BEFORE the snippet bodies are carved out (turn-stream.mjs:153-157, :163-170).
+  s = parseResponseSegments(W('<js>write("a.txt", "</remote-workspace>")</js>'));
+  bug(blocksOf(s).length === 1 && blocksOf(s)[0].calls[0].args.code === 'write("a.txt", "</remote-workspace>")' && !blocksOf(s)[0].calls[0].args.__unterminated,
+    'segments: a "</remote-workspace>" inside a snippet string must not cut the block (today: the call is cut and marked __unterminated)');
+  s = parseResponseSegments(W('<js>return grep("<workspace-result")</js>'));
+  bug(blocksOf(s).length === 1, 'segments: a "<workspace-result" inside a snippet string must not drop the block (today: the block becomes prose and never runs)');
+  // The result-tag cut ignores code spans, though the block search right after it masks them (turn-stream.mjs:153).
+  s = parseResponseSegments('Results come back in `<remote-workspace-result>` tags, which I then read.');
+  bug(proseOf(s).includes('which I then read'), 'segments: a result tag named in inline code must not truncate a prose answer (today: cut at the backtick)');
+  // The code-span mask pairs backticks ACROSS the block boundary: a snippet with an odd "```" (a markdown parser,
+  // say) pairs with a fence in the prose after it, the mask swallows </remote-workspace>, and the block is not run.
+  s = parseResponseSegments("<remote-workspace><js>const fence = '```';\nreturn 1</js></remote-workspace>\nHere:\n```js\nx\n```");
+  bug(blocksOf(s).length === 1, 'segments: an odd ``` inside a snippet must not hide the block close (today: the block becomes prose)');
+  s = parseResponseSegments('<remote-workspace><js>const s = `a\nb`; x</js></remote-workspace> and `y`');
+  bug(blocksOf(s).length === 1, 'segments: a multi-line template literal followed by inline code on the close line must not hide the block');
+
+  // volume
+  s = perf('parseResponseSegments 5 MB realistic', 2000, () => parseResponseSegments(R5.s));
+  ok(blocksOf(s).length === R5.blocks && blocksOf(s).every((b) => b.calls.length === 1), `segments 5 MB: all ${R5.blocks} blocks found, one call each`);
+  const blocks10k = Array.from({ length: 10000 }, (_, i) => `p${i} ` + W(`<js>${i}</js>`)).join('');
+  s = perf('parseResponseSegments 10,000 blocks', 2000, () => parseResponseSegments(blocks10k));
+  ok(blocksOf(s).length === 10000, 'segments: 10,000 blocks');
+  perf('parseResponseSegments 400k "``x " (unpaired double backticks)', 1000, () => parseResponseSegments('``x '.repeat(100000)));
+  perf('parseResponseSegments 4,000 "<remote-workspace " with no ">" (72 KB)', 150, () => parseResponseSegments('<remote-workspace '.repeat(4000)), true);
+}
+
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+// 4. LIVE PUMP — runTurnStreaming. The lexer pump (default, CB_TAG_LEXER on) and the regex pump are documented as
+//    "proven identical", and the live view must not diverge from what parseResponseSegments then executes.
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+async function stream(text, chunk, useTagLexer) {
+  const blocks = [], prose = [];
+  const t0 = performance.now();
+  const full = await runTurnStreaming({ async *completeStream() {
+    for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i += chunk) yield { choices: [{ delta: { content: text.slice(i, i + chunk) } }] };
+  } }, { signal: { aborted: false }, stallCutMs: 0 }, {
+    onBlockReady: ({ body }) => blocks.push(body), onProseChunk: ({ content }) => prose.push(content), useTagLexer,
+  });
+  return { full, blocks, prose: prose.join(''), ms: performance.now() - t0 };
+}
+{
+  const plain = 'Reading it.\n' + W('<js note="a>b">return 1</js>') + '\nDone.';
+  for (const lex of [true, false]) for (const sz of [1, 3, 64]) {
+    const r = await stream(plain, sz, lex);
+    ok(r.blocks.length === 1 && r.blocks[0] === '<js note="a>b">return 1</js>' && r.full === plain, `pump(${lex ? 'lexer' : 'regex'}, chunk ${sz}): one block, text intact`);
+  }
+  // The lexer pump re-tokenizes with atEof:false and never resolves an 'incomplete' tail at the end, so anything
+  // that tokenizes as incomplete (a stray backtick, an unterminated attr quote) or as a phantom tag that swallows
+  // the opener ("i <n" - section 1) hides the block from the live view: its code streams into the chat as prose.
+  // (turn-stream.mjs:458-471; the regex pump and parseResponseSegments both see the block.)
+  for (const [label, text] of [
+    ['"i <n" in prose', 'if i <n we stop.\n' + W('<js>return 1</js>')],
+    ['stray backtick in prose', "Don't type a ` here.\n" + W('<js>return 1</js>')],
+    ['unterminated attr quote in prose', 'Set <input value="5> as default.\n' + W('<js>return 1</js>')],
+  ]) {
+    const want = blocksOf(parseResponseSegments(text)).length;
+    const lex = await stream(text, 4, true), rx = await stream(text, 4, false);
+    note(`pump ${label}: blocks lexer/regex/segments`, [lex.blocks.length, rx.blocks.length, want]);
+    bug(lex.blocks.length === want, `pump(lexer): ${label} - the live pump must see the block parseResponseSegments runs (lexer ${lex.blocks.length}, segments ${want})`);
+    ok(rx.blocks.length === want, `pump(regex): ${label} - sees the block`);
+  }
+  // The stream is aborted at the first "<workspace-result" ANYWHERE (TURN_BOUNDARY_RE, turn-stream.mjs:107) - inside
+  // a snippet string or inline code too - contrary to the snippet-string protection tool-parser documents.
+  let r = await stream(W('<js>return grep("<workspace-result")</js>'), 8, true);
+  bug(r.blocks.length === 1, 'pump: a "<workspace-result" inside a snippet string must not cut the stream (today: reply truncated mid-snippet)');
+  r = await stream('Use `<remote-workspace-result>` here. Then more.', 8, true);
+  bug(r.full.includes('Then more.'), 'pump: a result tag named in inline code must not cut the stream');
+  // perf: in block state both pumps rescan from the block's body start on every chunk -> O(body^2 / chunk)
+  // (turn-stream.mjs:503 lexer, :440 regex). A 100 KB write in 25-char chunks:
+  const body = '<js>write("f.txt", "' + 'a'.repeat(100000) + '")</js>';
+  r = await stream(W(body), 25, true);
+  timings.push(`${r.ms.toFixed(0).padStart(6)} ms  (bound 500)  pump(lexer) 100 KB block in 25-char chunks`);
+  ok(r.blocks.length === 1 && r.blocks[0] === body, 'pump(lexer): 100 KB block delivered intact');
+  bug(r.ms < 500, `[PERF] pump(lexer): 100 KB block in 25-char chunks under 500 ms (took ${r.ms.toFixed(0)} ms)`);
+  r = await stream(W(body), 25, false);
+  timings.push(`${r.ms.toFixed(0).padStart(6)} ms  (recorded)    pump(regex) 100 KB block in 25-char chunks`);
+  ok(r.blocks.length === 1 && r.blocks[0] === body, 'pump(regex): 100 KB block delivered intact');
+}
+
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+// 5. TURN TAGS — turnTag: "Which turn tag a reply carries (code left out) ... A hand-over wins over the others."
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+{
+  for (const v of [undefined, null, '', '   ', 0]) eq(turnTag(v), null, `turnTag(${JSON.stringify(v)}) -> null`);
+  eq(turnTag('<hand-over>'), 'hand-over', 'turnTag: reply that is only the tag');
+  eq(turnTag('x <HAND-OVER/>'), 'hand-over', 'turnTag: case-insensitive, self-closing');
+  eq(turnTag('<waiting-on-subagent> <need-developer>'), 'hand-over', 'turnTag: hand-over wins');
+  eq(turnTag('<no-hand-over>'), 'no-hand-over', 'turnTag: <no-hand-over> is not read as <hand-over>');
+  eq(turnTag('<keep-turn>\r\n<waiting>'), 'waiting', 'turnTag: waiting beats no-hand-over, CRLF fine');
+  eq(turnTag('Use `<hand-over>` at the end.'), null, 'turnTag: inline code left out');
+  eq(turnTag('```\n<hand-over>\n```\n<keep-turn>'), 'no-hand-over', 'turnTag: fenced code left out');
+  eq(turnTag('<hand-overs>'), null, 'turnTag: a longer name is not the tag');
+  eq(turnTag('<hand-over x=">">'), 'hand-over', 'turnTag: attrs ok');
+  // "code left out" - but only single-backtick spans are removed; a ``double-backtick`` span is not (turn-tags.mjs:25)
+  bug(turnTag('Write ``<hand-over>`` to end your turn.') === null, 'turnTag: a tag inside a ``double-backtick`` code span is code and must be left out');
+  note('turnTag("<hand-over-notes>") (\\b after a hyphenated name)', turnTag('<hand-over-notes>'));
+  note('turnTag("<waiting-room>")', turnTag('<waiting-room>'));
+  note('turnTag("ok\\n<hand-over") (cut before ">")', turnTag('ok\n<hand-over'));
+  note('turnTag("```\\n<hand-over>") (unclosed fence)', turnTag('```\n<hand-over>'));
+  const r = perf('turnTag 5 MB realistic + tag', 1500, () => turnTag(R5.s + '\n<waiting>'));
+  eq(r, 'waiting', 'turnTag 5 MB: finds the tag at the end');
+  perf('turnTag 5,000 "<waiting " with no ">" (45 KB)', 150, () => turnTag('<waiting '.repeat(5000)), true);
+  ok('x <waiting a=1> y'.replace(TURN_TAG_RE, '') === 'x  y', 'TURN_TAG_RE strips a tag with attrs');
+}
+
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+// 6. PROSE SANITIZER — sanitizeAssistantProse (+ stripKeywords, truncateAtFirstBlock, holdLeadingRule)
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+const KV = { knownVerbs: ['js'] };
+{
+  // non-strings / empty are returned as-is
+  for (const v of [undefined, null, 0, '']) ok(sanitizeAssistantProse(v) === v, `sanitize(${JSON.stringify(v)}) returned as-is`);
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse(' \r\n\t '), '', 'sanitize: whitespace-only -> ""');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('<hand-over>'), '', 'sanitize: a reply that is only a turn tag -> ""');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('<hand-over>I need the port.</hand-over>'), 'I need the port.', 'sanitize: turn tag words kept');
+  // code-span protection: "a `<read>` inside backticks (an example the agent is SHOWING) survives"
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('Call `<js>x</js>` inside ```\n<js a=">">y</js>\n``` blocks.', KV), 'Call `<js>x</js>` inside ```\n<js a=">">y</js>\n``` blocks.', 'sanitize: verb tags inside code survive');
+  // quote-aware verb strip: "a `>` inside a quoted attr value never leaks a tag tail"
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('Before <js note="a > b">body</js> after <js t=\'>\'/> end <js x=">"> tail', KV), 'Before  after  end  tail', 'sanitize: quote-aware verb-tag strip (body, self-closing, bare)');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('Keep <library-ref>x</library-ref>.', KV), 'Keep <library-ref>x</library-ref>.', 'sanitize: unknown tags pass through');
+  // sentinel nonce: "a LITERAL «cbspan:0» typed in the prose can't collide"
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('Lit «cbspan:abc:0» and «cbspan:0» then `code`.'), 'Lit «cbspan:abc:0» and «cbspan:0» then `code`.', 'sanitize: literal sentinel text preserved');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('Put it in `<draft>`. <draft>secret</draft>Shown.'), 'Put it in `<draft>`. Shown.', 'sanitize: <draft> stripped, a draft NAMED in code kept');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('```\n<keywords>alpha, beta</keywords>\n```\nok <keywords>a, b</keywords>'), '```\n<keywords>alpha, beta</keywords>\n```\nok', 'sanitize: keywords in a fence kept, outside stripped');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('Hello\r\n\r\n---\r\n'), 'Hello', 'sanitize: CRLF trailing rule stripped');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('---\r\nHello'), 'Hello', 'sanitize: CRLF leading rule stripped');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('A\n\n---\n\nB'), 'A\n\n---\n\nB', 'sanitize: a rule in the middle survives');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('```\n---\n```'), '```\n---\n```', 'sanitize: a rule inside a fence is untouched');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('Done.\n\nAll set.'), 'Done.', 'sanitize: content-free closer stripped');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('Done.\n\nAll set.', { keepCloser: true }), 'Done.\n\nAll set.', 'sanitize: keepCloser');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('All set to deploy on Friday.'), 'All set to deploy on Friday.', 'sanitize: substantive sentence starting like a closer survives');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('Answer.\n/--- you ab12 ---\n--- developer ---'), 'Answer.', 'sanitize: echoed turn separators stripped');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('Answer 7f79e70.\n\n<key'), 'Answer 7f79e70.', 'sanitize: unfinished tag alone on the last line stripped');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('ok\n<remote-workspace-result'), 'ok', 'sanitize: cut-off result tag at the end stripped');
+  let s = sanitizeAssistantProse('x\0y \uD800 \u202Eabc \uD83D\uDE00 \uFEFFz');
+  eq(s, 'x\0y \uD800 \u202Eabc \uD83D\uDE00 \uFEFFz', 'sanitize: NUL, lone surrogate, RTL, emoji, inner BOM preserved');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('\uFEFF---\nHello'), 'Hello', 'sanitize: leading BOM + rule');
+  eq(sanitizeAssistantProse('Trail.\n\n`````````'), 'Trail.', 'sanitize: dangling empty backtick run stripped');
+  // recorded: the turn-tag strip runs BEFORE code-span protection, so a turn tag NAMED in code is removed from the
+  // displayed text too (TURN_TAG_RE's doc says "what the developer is never shown"; turnTag leaves code out).
+  note('sanitize("End with `<hand-over>` when you need me.")', sanitizeAssistantProse('End with `<hand-over>` when you need me.'));
+  note('sanitize("a\\n<b\\n<c") then again (not idempotent: one trailing partial tag per pass)', [sanitizeAssistantProse('a\n<b\n<c'), sanitizeAssistantProse(sanitizeAssistantProse('a\n<b\n<c'))]);
+
+  // idempotence over a corpus — not a stated contract, recorded only
+  const corpus = ['Done.\n\nAll set.', '---\nHello\n---', 'x `<hand-over>` y', 'Text\n\n```\n\n```', 'A ``` B', '<draft>x</draft> keep',
+    'ok <js a=">">b</js> end', 'Hi.\n\n```````', 'X\n\n---\n***\n', 'Let me redo that.\nReal.', '<mode>plan because</mode>Text', R1.s.slice(0, 20000)];
+  const notIdem = corpus.filter((t) => { const a = sanitizeAssistantProse(t, KV); return sanitizeAssistantProse(a, KV) !== a; });
+  note('sanitize not idempotent on', notIdem.map((t) => t.slice(0, 40)));
+
+  // volume
+  s = perf('sanitize 1 MB realistic', 1500, () => sanitizeAssistantProse(R1.s, KV));
+  s = perf('sanitize 5 MB realistic', 3000, () => sanitizeAssistantProse(R5.s, KV));
+  ok((s.match(/```js/g) || []).length === R5.fences, 'sanitize 5 MB: every fence kept');
+  ok(!/<remote-workspace/.test(s.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```|`[^`\n]*`/g, '')), 'sanitize 5 MB: no envelope tags left in prose');
+  ok(!/<js\b/.test(s.replace(/```[\s\S]*?```|`[^`\n]*`/g, '')), 'sanitize 5 MB: no verb tags left outside code');
+  perf('sanitize 1M x "<"', 1000, () => sanitizeAssistantProse('<'.repeat(1e6), KV));
+  perf('sanitize 250k x "<<<a"', 1000, () => sanitizeAssistantProse('<<<a'.repeat(250000), KV));
+  perf('sanitize 100k x "``x "', 1000, () => sanitizeAssistantProse('``x '.repeat(100000)));
+  perf('sanitize 1 MB dash line + x', 1000, () => sanitizeAssistantProse('start\n' + '-'.repeat(1e6) + 'x'));
+  perf('sanitize 10,000 small verb tags', 1000, () => sanitizeAssistantProse('a <js t="1">b</js> c '.repeat(10000), KV));
+  // pathological, quadratic today:
+  perf('sanitize 4,000 "<waiting " with no ">" (36 KB)', 150, () => sanitizeAssistantProse('<waiting '.repeat(4000)), true);       // TURN_TAGS_RE [^>]*> retried per start
+  perf('sanitize 1,500 "<js a=\\"x " unterminated quotes (14 KB)', 150, () => sanitizeAssistantProse('<js a="x '.repeat(1500), KV), true);  // verb ATTR run + [\s\S]*?</\1>
+  perf('sanitize 2,500 consecutive "---" lines mid-text (10 KB)', 150, () => sanitizeAssistantProse('start\n' + '---\n'.repeat(2500) + 'end'), true);  // trailing-rule regex, prose-sanitizer.mjs:316
+
+  // stripKeywords / truncateAtFirstBlock / holdLeadingRule
+  eq(stripKeywords('a <keywords>x, y</keywords> b <keywords>open to end'), 'a  b ', 'stripKeywords: closed and open-to-end lists');
+  perf('stripKeywords 5 MB', 1000, () => stripKeywords(R5.s + '<keywords>k</keywords>'));
+  for (const t of ['a', '<remote-workspace><js>x</js></remote-workspace> tail', 'p <remote-workspace><js>x</js><remote-workspace-result>F', 'x <workspace-result>y', R1.s]) {
+    const once = truncateAtFirstBlock(t);
+    ok(truncateAtFirstBlock(once) === once, `truncateAtFirstBlock idempotent on ${JSON.stringify(t.slice(0, 30))}`);
+  }
+  eq(truncateAtFirstBlock('p <remote-workspace><js>x</js><remote-workspace-result>F'), 'p <remote-workspace><js>x</js>\n</remote-workspace>', 'truncateAtFirstBlock: closes a block cut by a result tag');
+  // "Truncate a response to just the first REAL <remote-workspace> block" - a close named in inline code is not
+  // one; used for the persisted history of a prose-only reply (minimal-worker.mjs:2244), it cuts the answer.
+  bug(truncateAtFirstBlock('To close a block write `</remote-workspace>`. Then more explanation.').includes('Then more explanation.'),
+    'truncateAtFirstBlock: a close tag named in inline code must not truncate a prose reply');
+  perf('truncateAtFirstBlock 5 MB', 500, () => truncateAtFirstBlock(R5.s));
+  eq(holdLeadingRule(''), { hold: true }, 'holdLeadingRule: empty holds');
+  eq(holdLeadingRule('--'), { hold: true }, 'holdLeadingRule: partial rule holds');
+  eq(holdLeadingRule('---\r\nText'), { hold: false, emit: 'Text' }, 'holdLeadingRule: CRLF rule dropped');
+  eq(holdLeadingRule('- item'), { hold: false, emit: '- item' }, 'holdLeadingRule: a list item is released');
+  perf('holdLeadingRule 1M "- " partial', 500, () => holdLeadingRule('- '.repeat(500000)));
+}
+
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+// 7. LIVE PROSE FILTER — createLiveProseFilter: "keeps a tail that MAY STILL BECOME one of them (a "<no-hand",
+//    a line "/--- yo") for the next chunk, and drops the whole ones."
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+const liveRun = (text, size) => { const f = createLiveProseFilter(); let out = ''; for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i += size) out += f(text.slice(i, i + size)); return out; };
+{
+  for (const t of ['a <hand-over> b', 'x <no-hand-over/> y', 'q <keywords>a, b</keywords> r', '```\n<keywords>k</keywords>\n```\nz', 'a <waiting x=">"> c', 'pre <b>bold</b> post']) {
+    const one = createLiveProseFilter()(t);
+    for (const sz of [1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 64]) eq(liveRun(t, sz), one, `live filter: ${JSON.stringify(t)} chunk ${sz} == one-shot`);
+  }
+  eq(createLiveProseFilter()('x <hand-over> y <waiting/>'), 'x  y ', 'live filter: whole turn tags dropped');
+  note('live filter "one\\n/--- you ab12 ---\\n" chunk 1 vs one-shot (extra blank line)', [liveRun('one\n/--- you ab12 ---\n', 1), createLiveProseFilter()('one\n/--- you ab12 ---\n')]);
+  // A bare "<" (a comparison) can never become a turn tag, but the partial-tag regex /<\/?([a-z-]*)(?:[\s/][^<>]*)?$/
+  // takes an EMPTY name followed by a space as a possible one (prose-sanitizer.mjs:135), so everything after
+  // "a < b" is held back from the live view until a later "<" or ">" arrives - and each chunk re-scans the
+  // growing carry.
+  bug(liveRun('Since a < b holds, the loop ends. More text follows here.', 4).includes('More text'), 'live filter: prose after "a < b" must stream (today: held until the segment ends)');
+  bug(liveRun('Loop while i <n holds. More text follows here.', 4).includes('More text'), 'live filter: prose after "i <n" must stream');
+  perf('live filter 5 MB realistic in 64-char chunks', 2000, () => liveRun(R5.s, 64));
+  perf('live filter 100 KB after a bare "<" in 50-char chunks', 150, () => liveRun('a < b. ' + 'More plain words here. '.repeat(4400), 50), true);
+}
+
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+// 8. LEADING THOUGHT — splitLeadingThought / maybeThoughtHead
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+{
+  eq(splitLeadingThought('no header'), null, 'splitLeadingThought: no header -> null');
+  eq(splitLeadingThought(''), null, 'splitLeadingThought: empty -> null');
+  let r = splitLeadingThought('\uD83E\uDEE7thought\r\nCheck it.The answer is 4.', { final: true });
+  ok(r && r.seam === 'glued' && r.answer === 'The answer is 4.', 'splitLeadingThought: CRLF header, glued seam');
+  r = splitLeadingThought('thought\nI see `a.B` there.Answer', { final: true });
+  ok(r && r.answer === 'Answer', 'splitLeadingThought: a joint inside inline code is not the seam');
+  r = splitLeadingThought('thought\nthinking more\n<remote-workspace><js>x</js></remote-workspace>');
+  ok(r && r.seam === 'block' && r.answer.startsWith('<remote-workspace>'), 'splitLeadingThought: tool block is the seam');
+  ok(maybeThoughtHead('thou') && maybeThoughtHead('\uD83E\uDEE7') && !maybeThoughtHead('the'), 'maybeThoughtHead: partial headers');
+  const long = 'thought\n' + 'Hmm. ok. '.repeat(100000) + 'done.The answer';
+  r = perf('splitLeadingThought 900 KB thought', 1000, () => splitLeadingThought(long, { final: true }));
+  ok(r && r.answer === 'The answer', 'splitLeadingThought 900 KB: seam found');
+  const bounds = []; for (let i = 8; i < long.length; i += 40) bounds.push(i);
+  perf('splitLeadingThought 900 KB with 22k chunk bounds', 1000, () => splitLeadingThought(long, { bounds, final: true }));
+  // outsideCode(s, i) counts every backtick from 0 on each candidate (prose-sanitizer.mjs:178); after an odd
+  // backtick every candidate is "in code", so each one re-scans the prefix -> O(n^2).
+  perf('splitLeadingThought 35 KB thought after an odd backtick', 150, () => splitLeadingThought('thought\nLooking at `the code ' + 'list.Add more '.repeat(2500), { final: true }), true);
+}
+
+// ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+console.log('\nTimings:');
+for (const t of timings) console.log('  ' + t);
+console.log('\nObserved (recorded behaviour, not asserted):');
+for (const o of observed) console.log('  - ' + o);
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+if (fails.length) { console.log('FAILURES:'); for (const f of fails) console.log('  ✗ ' + f); }
+process.exit(fail === 0 ? 0 : 1);
