=== add test/test-tokens-edges-volume.mjs
new 593f3bb8c7347c5ddf9c7e05
+/**
+ * The token / masking layer under EDGE CASES and VOLUME (2026-11-18). What production runs: the token store
+ * (src/tokens.mjs), the platform's own pass and the plugin pass (src/plugins/filters.mjs), the shipped piishape loaded
+ * by the plugin manager, big texts on the masking thread (src/plugins/filter-worker.mjs), and the helpers around them
+ * (src/agent/known-values.mjs, src/agent/pii-return.mjs, src/agent/mask-segments.mjs).
+ *   [1] the grammar: malformed and forged tokens, huge ids, missing closers, cutTokenSafe never halves a token,
+ *       tokenFormatOf / valueInFormat round trips over awkward unicode, the id (case, NFC/NFD, grouped digits)
+ *   [2] piishape round trips: unicode, emoji, RTL, combining marks, CRLF, punctuation, case, many times, empty
+ *   [3] the platform pass (issued hidden values): overlapping, substrings, many times, regex characters, token-like
+ *       values, emoji and CRLF values, the too-plain-to-hunt rule (<3 chars, <=3 digits) - and where a value stands
+ *       after '.', '-', '_' inside a URL or a path
+ *   [4] forged tokens: data in a shape token's id / length slot, in a known named token's format slot, in piiscan's
+ *       legacy type slot; an unknown named token unframed; unknown on the way back refused; a value split across two
+ *       mask calls; a file part masked with the lines before it
+ *   [5] tokens in code / JSON strings: escaping on the way back; a JSON file written with \uXXXX escapes
+ *   [6] limits: just under / at / over the worker threshold (64 KB) - the same mask on either thread, and a token
+ *       issued on the worker resolves here; just under / at / over piishape's 2 MB cap - refused, nothing passed
+ *       through, on this thread and on the worker; the cap is UTF-16 code units; turn markers split the cap
+ *   [7] volume: 10,000 distinct issued values; 1 MB of mixed text with 10,000 distinct names; 2 MB at the cap;
+ *       1 MB of near-miss tokens; a 200 KB JSON through maskReturn - each timed, memory watched
+ *   [8] determinism: the same input, the same tokens; the same id in both token families; another secret, other ids;
+ *       a second session (a fresh piishape) resolves the first session's tokens
+ *   [9] known-values: kvPairs / kvApply at edges and at their cap
+ * A check labelled "BUG" fails because of a product bug (see the report); it is left failing on purpose.
+ * Run: node test/test-tokens-edges-volume.mjs
+ */
+import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
+import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
+import path from 'node:path';
+import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
+const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
+process.env.CB_HOME = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-tokedge-'));
+const T = await import('../src/tokens.mjs');
+const { createTokenStore, parseToken, tokenIdOf, tokenFormatOf, valueInFormat, cutTokenSafe, TOKEN_RE, ANY_TOKEN_SRC, ANY_TOKEN_WHOLE_RE, escapedSpellings } = T;
+const { createFilterPipeline, FilterError } = await import('../src/plugins/filters.mjs');
+const { createFilterWorkers, FILTER_WORKER_MIN } = await import('../src/plugins/filter-worker.mjs');
+const { createPluginManager } = await import('../src/plugins/index.mjs');
+const { kvPairs, kvApply } = await import('../src/agent/known-values.mjs');
+const { maskReturn } = await import('../src/agent/pii-return.mjs');
+const { maskSearchLines, maskByFileContext } = await import('../src/agent/mask-segments.mjs');
+
+let pass = 0, fail = 0;
+const failures = [];
+const ok = (c, l) => { if (c) { pass++; console.log('  ok', l); } else { fail++; failures.push(l); console.log('  FAIL', l); } };
+const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));
+const short = (s, n = 160) => { const t = JSON.stringify(String(s)); return t.length > n ? t.slice(0, n) + '…' : t; };
+const MB = 1024 * 1024;
+const CAP = 2 * MB;   // piishape's MAX_MASK_BYTES (examples/plugins/piishape__1.mjs)
+const timed = async (fn) => { const t0 = Date.now(); const v = await fn(); return [v, Date.now() - t0]; };
+const tryErr = async (fn) => { try { return [await fn(), null]; } catch (e) { return [null, e]; } };
+
+// ── setup: what production runs ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
+const pdir = path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'plugins');
+mkdirSync(pdir, { recursive: true });
+const PS_SRC = readFileSync(path.join(ROOT, 'examples', 'plugins', 'piishape__1.mjs'), 'utf8');
+writeFileSync(path.join(pdir, 'piishape__1.mjs'), PS_SRC);
+const SECRET = 'edge-secret';
+const store = createTokenStore({ secret: SECRET });
+const logs = [];
+const pm = createPluginManager({ onLog: (m) => logs.push(String(m)), onHostRequest: async () => ({}), tokens: store });
+pm.setPluginsDir(pdir);
+await pm.reconcile();
+const workers = createFilterWorkers({ log: (m) => logs.push(String(m)) });
+const pipeOpts = { ownNames: () => pm.exemptNames(), namesVersion: () => pm.namesVersion(), tokens: store };
+const pipe = createFilterPipeline(() => pm.enabledFilters(), { ...pipeOpts, workers });   // big texts on the worker
+const pipeHere = createFilterPipeline(() => pm.enabledFilters(), pipeOpts);              // everything on this thread
+const storeOnly = createFilterPipeline(() => [], { tokens: store });                      // the platform pass alone
+const proj = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'cb-tokedge-proj-'));
+const mask = (t, extra = {}) => pipe.mask(t, { hook: 'tool-result', projectDir: proj, ...extra });
+const maskHere = (t, extra = {}) => pipeHere.mask(t, { hook: 'tool-result', projectDir: proj, ...extra });
+const unmask = (t, extra = {}) => pipe.unmask(t, { hook: 'tool-args', projectDir: proj, toolName: 'write', ...extra });
+const roundTrip = async (t, extra = {}) => { const m = await mask(t, extra); const [u, e] = await tryErr(() => unmask(m, extra)); return { m, u, e }; };
+const tokensIn = (s) => [...String(s).matchAll(new RegExp(ANY_TOKEN_SRC, 'gu'))].map((x) => x[0]);
+ok(pm.enabledFilters().some((f) => f.name === 'piishape' && f.worker === true && f.pluginFile), 'setup: the shipped piishape is loaded, a worker filter');
+
+try {
+  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  console.log('\n[1] the grammar');
+  {
+    const P = (s) => parseToken(s);
+    ok(P('#Xxxxx#abcdefg') && P('#Xxxxx#abcdefg').id === 'abcdefg', 'a shape token with an id no one issued still PARSES (validation is the resolver\'s job)');
+    ok(P('#Xxxxx#abcdefgh') === null && P('#Xxxxx#abcdef') === null, 'an id of 8 or 6 characters is not a token');
+    ok(P('#Xxxxx#' + 'a'.repeat(5000)) === null && tokensIn('#Xxxxx#' + 'a'.repeat(5000)).length === 0, 'a huge id is not a token, and no token is found inside it');
+    ok(P('#Xxxxx#ABCDEFG') === null && tokensIn('#Xxxxx#ABCDEFG').length === 0, 'an upper-case id is not a token');
+    ok(P('#Xxxxx') === null && P('#Xxxxx#') === null && P('Xxxxx#abcdefg') === null && P('#@ref#') === null && P('#@#abcdefg') === null, 'missing opener / closer / id / name: not tokens');
+    ok(tokensIn('a #Xxxxx#abcdefgé b').length === 0 && tokensIn('a #Xxxxx#abcdefg1 b').length === 0, 'a letter or digit (any script) after the id ends it as a non-token');
+    ok(tokensIn('(#Xxxxx#abcdefg).').length === 1 && tokensIn('"#@a.b#abcdefg"').length === 1, 'punctuation around a token is fine');
+    ok(P('#num~@a.b~000#abcdefg') && P('#num~@a.b~000#abcdefg').num === true && P('#num~@a.b~000#abcdefg').name === 'a.b', 'a number token parses, name without format');
+    ok(P('#num~num~Xx#abcdefg') === null, 'num~ twice is not a token');
+    ok(P('#@' + 'n'.repeat(81) + '#abcdefg') && P('#@' + 'n'.repeat(82) + '#abcdefg') === null, 'a name of 81 characters is one, 82 is not');
+    ok(P('#X...123456789#abcdefg') && P('#X...1234567890#abcdefg') === null && P('#X…0#abcdefg') === null, 'a shortened shape: 1-9 digits of length, not starting 0');
+    ok(ANY_TOKEN_WHOLE_RE.test('#Xx#abcdefg') && !ANY_TOKEN_WHOLE_RE.test('x #Xx#abcdefg') && !ANY_TOKEN_WHOLE_RE.test('#Xx#abcdefg x'), 'ANY_TOKEN_WHOLE_RE: a whole token only');
+    // a format slot can be very long; matching must stay linear
+    const longFmt = '#@a~' + 'x'.repeat(500000) + '#abcdefg';
+    const [m1, ms1] = await timed(() => tokensIn(longFmt));
+    ok(m1.length === 1 && ms1 < 2000, `a 500,000-character format slot is one token, found in ${ms1} ms`);
+    const [m2, ms2] = await timed(() => tokensIn(('#@a~' + 'x'.repeat(5000) + '#abcdef ').repeat(100)));
+    ok(m2.length === 0 && ms2 < 3000, `100 near-misses with 5,000-character format slots: none, in ${ms2} ms`);
+
+    // cutTokenSafe: at EVERY cut, no token in the text is cut in half
+    const text = 'abc #@smiths.sponsor.ssn~000-00-0000#k2j4q7m d #Xxxx#abcdefg, @@@(a.b) [PII:SSN#a1b2c3d4] #000-00-0000#zzzzzzz end';
+    const spans = [...text.matchAll(new RegExp(ANY_TOKEN_SRC, 'gu'))].map((m) => [m.index, m.index + m[0].length]);
+    let bad = null;
+    for (let n = 1; n <= text.length + 2; n++) {
+      const out = cutTokenSafe(text, n);
+      if (out.length > Math.max(n, 1)) { bad = `n=${n}: longer than n (${out.length})`; break; }
+      const body = out.length === text.length && out === text ? out : out.slice(0, -1);
+      const c = body.length;
+      if (!text.startsWith(body)) { bad = `n=${n}: not a prefix`; break; }
+      if (spans.some(([a, b]) => a < c && c < b)) { bad = `n=${n}: cut inside a token (${short(out)})`; break; }
+    }
+    ok(bad === null, `cutTokenSafe never halves a token, at every one of ${text.length + 2} cut points${bad ? ' - ' + bad : ''}`);
+    ok(cutTokenSafe('', 5) === '' && cutTokenSafe(null, 5) === '' && cutTokenSafe('abc', 3) === 'abc', 'cutTokenSafe: empty, null, exact length');
+
+    // tokenFormatOf → valueInFormat is exact for awkward values
+    const awkward = ['Zoë Ångström', 'José', 'Jose\u0301', 'محمد علي', 'שלום', '李小龙', '😀Bob👨‍👩‍👧', 'İSTANBUL', 'straße', 'line1\r\nline2', 'tab\there', "O'Neil \"Q\" `b` \\c", 'a#b', '50%23 off', '%00', '${x}', '١٢٣-٤٥', '１２３', 'x²½', ' lead and trail ', 'a\u00a0b\u2028c', '\u202Eevil\u202C'];
+    let wrong = [];
+    for (const v of awkward) { const f = tokenFormatOf(v); if (valueInFormat(v, f) !== v) wrong.push(`${short(v, 30)} → ${short(f, 30)} → ${short(valueInFormat(v, f), 30)}`); }
+    ok(wrong.length === 0, `tokenFormatOf → valueInFormat gives back every awkward value (${wrong.join('; ') || awkward.length + ' values'})`);
+    // a named token built for each must parse back to its name and format, and match the grammar whole
+    wrong = [];
+    for (const v of awkward) { const t = T.namedTokenFor('t.v', tokenIdOf(v, SECRET), v); const p = parseToken(t); if (!p || p.name !== 't.v' || valueInFormat(v, p.format) !== v || tokensIn('x ' + t + ' y')[0] !== t) wrong.push(short(v, 30) + ' → ' + short(t, 50)); }
+    ok(wrong.length === 0, `a named token for each awkward value is one whole token that parses back (${wrong.join('; ') || 'all'})`);
+    // the format must not carry the value's characters (letters / digits) - what it does carry, recorded
+    const carried = awkward.map((v) => [v, tokenFormatOf(v).replace(/%[0-9A-F]{2}/g, '').replace(/[Xx0␣]/g, '')]).filter(([, rest]) => /[\p{L}\p{N}]/u.test(rest));
+    ok(carried.length === 0, `BUG(low): no letter or digit of a value is copied into its format - tokenFormatOf maps only \\p{Nd} to 0, so \\p{No}/\\p{Nl} numerals pass as they are (copied:${carried.map(([v, r]) => short(v, 20) + '→' + short(r, 20)).join('; ') || 'none'})`);
+    // the id
+    ok(tokenIdOf('SMITH', SECRET) === tokenIdOf('smith', SECRET), 'the id ignores case');
+    ok(tokenIdOf('José', SECRET) === tokenIdOf('Jose\u0301', SECRET), 'NFC and NFD spellings share one id');
+    ok(tokenIdOf('123-45-6789', SECRET) === tokenIdOf('123456789', SECRET) && tokenIdOf('123 45 6789', SECRET) === tokenIdOf('123.45.6789', SECRET), 'a number in groups has its digits\' id');
+    ok(tokenIdOf('İSTANBUL', SECRET) !== tokenIdOf('istanbul', SECRET), 'recorded: "İSTANBUL" and "istanbul" have different ids (toLowerCase of İ is i + a combining dot)');
+    ok(tokenIdOf('x', SECRET) === tokenIdOf('x', SECRET) && tokenIdOf('x', SECRET) !== tokenIdOf('x', SECRET + '2'), 'deterministic, and the secret is in it');
+    const ids = new Set(); for (let i = 0; i < 20000; i++) ids.add(tokenIdOf('value-' + i, SECRET));
+    ok(ids.size >= 19990, `20,000 values, ${20000 - ids.size} id collisions (36^7 ids)`);
+    ok(escapedSpellings('plain').length === 0 && escapedSpellings('a"b').includes('a\\"b'), 'escapedSpellings: none for a plain value, the JSON one for a quote');
+  }
+
+  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  console.log('\n[2] piishape round trips (mask → unmask is exact, nothing personal left)');
+  {
+    const cases = [
+      ['unicode names', 'Patient Zoë Ångström-Øberg and 李小龙 and Müller-Lüdenscheidt', ['Ångström', 'Øberg', '李小龙', 'Lüdenscheidt']],
+      ['combining marks (NFD)', 'Jose\u0301 Garci\u0301a, then José García (NFC)', ['Garci', 'García']],
+      ['emoji around and inside', 'Contact 😀Bobbington😀 at 👨‍👩‍👧 Smithers-Qavanagh ✔️', ['Bobbington', 'Smithers', 'Qavanagh']],
+      ['RTL text and overrides', 'name: محمد علي, שלום כהן, \u202EKowalczyk\u202C', ['محمد', 'כהן', 'Kowalczyk']],
+      ['CRLF lines', 'John Qavanagh\r\nJane Kowalczyk\r\n\r\nend\r\n', ['Qavanagh', 'Kowalczyk']],
+      ['punctuation all around', '(Zqhavisham), "Zqhavisham"; [Zqhavisham]. {Zqhavisham}! <Zqhavisham>? \'Zqhavisham\'', ['Zqhavisham']],
+      ['case variants', 'Qavanagh QAVANAGH qavanagh', ['Qavanagh', 'QAVANAGH', 'qavanagh']],
+      ['QUESTIONABLE (piishape heuristic: an identifier of parts of 2 letters or fewer is shown) odd case', 'sMiTh and qAvAnAgH', ['qAvAnAgH']],
+      ['numbers', 'ssn 123-45-6789, phone (555) 867-5309, card 4111 1111 1111 1111, zip 75201-1188', ['6789', '867', '5309', '4111', '75201']],
+      ['a hash in the text', 'issue #42 and ## heading and C# and #hashtag Zqhavisham', ['Zqhavisham']],
+      ['tabs and odd spaces', 'Name:\tQavanagh\u00a0Kowalczyk\u2003Zqhavisham\u3000end', ['Qavanagh', 'Kowalczyk', 'Zqhavisham']],
+    ];
+    for (const [label, t, secrets] of cases) {
+      const { m, u, e } = await roundTrip(t);
+      const leaked = secrets.filter((s) => m.includes(s));
+      ok(!e && u === t, `${label}: round trip exact${e ? ' - refused: ' + e.message.slice(0, 120) : u !== t ? ' - got ' + short(u) : ''}`);
+      ok(leaked.length === 0, `${label}: nothing personal in what the model gets${leaked.length ? ' - LEAKED ' + leaked.join(', ') + ' in ' + short(m) : ''}`);
+    }
+    // the same value many times
+    const many = Array.from({ length: 5000 }, (_, i) => (i % 2 ? 'Zqhavisham' : 'ZQHAVISHAM')).join(', ');
+    const { m: mm, u: mu } = await roundTrip(many);
+    ok(!/zqhavisham/i.test(mm) && new Set(tokensIn(mm).map((t) => parseToken(t).id)).size === 1 && mu === many, `the same value 5,000 times: one id, round trip exact (${mm.length} chars)`);
+    // empty and whitespace-only
+    ok((await mask('')) === '' && (await unmask('')) === '' && (await mask('   \r\n\t ')) === '   \r\n\t ', 'empty and whitespace-only text pass as they are');
+    // idempotent
+    const t = 'Patient Zoë Ångström, ssn 123-45-6789, Jose\u0301';
+    const once = await mask(t);
+    ok((await mask(once)) === once, 'masking twice changes nothing');
+  }
+
+  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  console.log('\n[3] the platform pass: issued hidden values');
+  {
+    const iss = (v, name, kind = 'family') => store.issue(v, { name, kind, plugin: 'pii-testdata', ttlDays: 3650 });
+    const V = {
+      last: iss('Zqhavisham', 'p.last'), first: iss('Johnathon', 'p.first'), full: iss('Johnathon Zqhavisham', 'p.full'),
+      st: iss('blue river', 'p.street'), s1: iss('Qorvath', 'q.last'), s2: iss('Qorvathson', 'q.last2'),
+      phone: iss('555-867-5309', 'p.phone'), weird: iss('a+b(c)*[d]$^', 'p.weird', 'value'),
+      tokenish: iss('#Xxxxx#abcdefg', 'p.tokenish', 'value'), emoji: iss('Zoë 😀', 'p.emoji', 'value'),
+      crlf: iss('line1\r\nline2', 'p.crlf', 'value'), two: iss('42', 'p.two', 'value'), three: iss('007', 'p.three', 'value'),
+      ab: iss('ab', 'p.ab', 'value'), quote: iss('said "Qorvath" twice', 'p.quote', 'value'), nfc: iss('Jösé Qarñez', 'p.nfc', 'value'),
+    };
+    const SO = (t) => storeOnly.mask(t, { hook: 'tool-result' });
+    const out1 = await SO('Johnathon Zqhavisham, Johnathon, Zqhavisham; JohnathonZqhavisham');
+    ok(out1.startsWith(V.full + ', ' + V.first + ', ' + V.last + ';') && out1.endsWith('JohnathonZqhavisham'), `overlapping values: the longest wins, each alone gets its own, a run-together word is left to piishape (${short(out1, 200)})`);
+    const out2 = await SO('Qorvath Qorvathson Qorvathsonian');
+    ok(out2 === `${V.s1} ${V.s2} Qorvathsonian`, `a value that is a substring of another: each whole word its own token (${short(out2, 200)})`);
+    const many = Array.from({ length: 3000 }, () => 'Zqhavisham').join(' / ');
+    const outMany = await SO(many);
+    ok(!outMany.includes('Zqhavisham') && tokensIn(outMany).length === 3000, 'the same issued value 3,000 times: all 3,000 masked');
+    const out3 = await SO('call 555-867-5309, 5558675309, 555.867.5309, 555 867 5309; x a+b(c)*[d]$^ y');
+    ok(!/867|5309/.test(out3) && out3.includes(V.weird), `a phone in every spelling; regex characters in a value (${short(out3, 220)})`);
+    const out4 = await SO('note #Xxxxx#abcdefg here; hi Zoë 😀 there; a line1\r\nline2 b');
+    ok(out4.includes(parseToken(V.tokenish).id) && !out4.includes('#Xxxxx#abcdefg') && out4.includes(parseToken(V.emoji).id) && out4.includes(parseToken(V.crlf).id), `a token-like value, an emoji value, a CRLF value are masked (${short(out4, 220)})`);
+    const out5 = await SO('there are 42 rows, agent 007, ab testing');
+    ok(out5 === 'there are 42 rows, agent 007, ab testing', 'too plain to hunt: a value under 3 characters or of 3 digits or fewer is not looked for in other text (documented)');
+    const out6 = await SO(JSON.stringify({ q: 'said "Qorvath" twice' }));
+    ok(!out6.includes('Qorvath'), `a value with quotes, in its JSON spelling (${short(out6)})`);
+    // whole round trip with piishape after the store pass
+    for (const t of ['Johnathon Zqhavisham, Johnathon, Zqhavisham; JohnathonZqhavisham', 'call 555-867-5309 or 5558675309', 'hi Zoë 😀 there, a line1\r\nline2 b', 'note #Xxxxx#abcdefg here']) {
+      const { u, e } = await roundTrip(t);
+      ok(!e && u === t, `round trip through both passes: ${short(t, 60)}${e ? ' - refused: ' + e.message.slice(0, 100) : u !== t ? ' - got ' + short(u) : ''}`);
+    }
+    // NFD spelling of an NFC value: the platform pass looks for the NFC characters only; piishape is the backstop
+    const nfd = 'Jösé Qarñez'.normalize('NFD');
+    const mNfd = await mask('client ' + nfd + ' signed');
+    ok(!/Q\s*a\s*r/u.test(mNfd.normalize('NFC').replace(/#[^#]*#[0-9a-z]{7}/g, '')) && !mNfd.normalize('NFC').includes('Qarñez'), `an issued value in its NFD spelling does not reach the model (${short(mNfd)})`);
+    // ...but where piishape SHOWS the words (a value of common words), the NFD spelling gets half through
+    store.issue('café rouge', { name: 'p.nfcwords', kind: 'value', plugin: 'x', ttlDays: 30 });
+    ok(!(await mask('we had café rouge today')).includes('rouge'), 'contrast: an issued value of common words, NFC, is masked whole');
+    const mNfdWords = await mask('we had ' + 'café rouge'.normalize('NFD') + ' today');
+    ok(!mNfdWords.includes('rouge'), `BUG(partial leak): the same issued value in its NFD spelling is masked whole - the platform pass matches the NFC characters only - got ${short(mNfdWords)}`);
+
+    // A VALUE AFTER '.', '-' OR '_': the platform pass will not match it (filters.mjs, the lookbehind
+    // (?<![\p{L}\p{N}_#@.-]) meant for "never inside a token"), and piishape SHOWS URLs and path-like spans by design.
+    // So an issued hidden value in a URL or a path after one of those characters reaches the model as it is.
+    const cases = [
+      ['a LinkedIn-style URL', 'profile https://linkedin.com/in/jane-Zqhavisham today'],
+      ['a URL path segment after a dot', 'see https://example.com/u/a.Zqhavisham now'],
+      ['a URL after an underscore', 'see https://example.com/u/user_Zqhavisham now'],
+      ['a Windows path', 'opened C:\\Users\\jane.Zqhavisham\\notes.txt'],
+      ['a POSIX path', 'opened /home/jane-Zqhavisham/notes.txt'],
+    ];
+    for (const [label, t] of cases) {
+      const m = await mask(t);
+      ok(!m.includes('Zqhavisham'), `BUG(LEAK): an issued hidden value in ${label} is masked - got ${short(m)}`);
+    }
+    // the same with a slash before it, for contrast: masked
+    ok(!(await mask('https://example.com/people/Zqhavisham/profile')).includes('Zqhavisham'), 'contrast: after a "/" in a URL it is masked');
+    ok(!(await mask('cloned git@github.com:jane-Zqhavisham/repo.git')).includes('Zqhavisham'), 'contrast: in a git remote (not a URL piishape keeps) piishape masks it');
+    // a common-words value after a '-' / '.': only half of it is caught
+    const half = await mask('see -blue river and x.blue river');
+    ok(!/\briver\b/.test(half), `BUG(partial leak): an issued value of common words after "-" or "." is masked whole - got ${short(half)}`);
+  }
+
+  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  console.log('\n[4] forged and malformed tokens');
+  {
+    // A token typed by a user: kept by piishape (it keeps "tokens already in the text"), refused on the way back
+    const typed = 'the docs say #Xxxxx#zzzzzzz is a token';
+    const { m, e } = await roundTrip(typed);
+    ok(m === typed, 'a shape token nobody issued, typed in the text, passes the mask as it is (piishape keeps token-shaped spans)');
+    ok(e instanceof FilterError && /not a token this session issued/.test(e.message), 'on the way back it is refused (fail-closed), not typed in');
+    const ex = await roundTrip('example #Xxxx0#k2j4q7m');
+    ok(!ex.e && ex.u === 'example #Xxxx0#k2j4q7m', 'a worked example\'s id (k2j4q7m) round trips as text');
+
+    // DATA IN A SHAPE TOKEN'S SLOTS: the id is 7 free characters and a shortened shape's length is 9 free digits.
+    // piishape keeps any token-shaped span whole, issued or not, so what is in those slots is never looked at.
+    for (const [label, t, secret] of [
+      ['a 9-digit SSN in a shortened shape\'s length slot', 'record #0...123456789#abcdefg', '123456789'],
+      ['7 digits of a phone in the id slot', 'call #0#8675309 now', '8675309'],
+      ['a name in the id slot', 'owner #X#zqhavis done', 'zqhavis'],
+    ]) {
+      const mm = await mask(t);
+      ok(!mm.includes(secret), `BUG(LEAK, low): ${label} is masked - got ${short(mm)}`);
+    }
+    // DATA IN A KNOWN NAMED TOKEN'S FORMAT SLOT: the store knows the name and id, so the token is kept whole - and
+    // the format is never checked against the value (a real format holds only X x 0, separators and %XX)
+    const last = parseToken(store.issue('Zqhavisham', { name: 'p.last', kind: 'family', plugin: 'pii-testdata', ttlDays: 3650 }));
+    for (const [label, fmt, secret] of [['another person\'s name', 'JonasKowalczyk', 'Kowalczyk'], ['an SSN', '123-45-6789', '6789'], ['the hidden value itself', 'Zqhavisham', 'Zqhavisham']]) {
+      const mm = await mask(`see #@p.last~${fmt}#${last.id} ok`);
+      ok(!mm.includes(secret), `BUG(LEAK, low): ${label} in the format slot of a known named token is masked - got ${short(mm)}`);
+    }
+    // ...and the same token comes back as the stored value (the format does not line up: never invented)
+    ok((await unmask(`see #@p.last~JonasKowalczyk#${last.id} ok`)) === 'see Zqhavisham ok', 'a forged format resolves to the value as stored, never to the format');
+    // LEGACY [PII:TYPE#hex] is left alone by the platform pass and kept by piishape - its slots carry what they carry
+    for (const [label, t, secret] of [['a name in its type slot', 'old [PII:Zqhavisham#a1b2c3] row', 'Zqhavisham'], ['an SSN in its hex slot', 'old [PII:SSN#123456789] row', '123456789']]) {
+      const mm = await mask(t);
+      ok(!mm.includes(secret), `BUG(LEAK, low): ${label} of a legacy [PII:…] token is masked - got ${short(mm)}`);
+    }
+    // an unknown NAMED token is unframed and masked as text (documented) - the round trip loses its leading #
+    const fake = 'see #@nobody~JonasKowalczyk#abcdefg and #num~@x~Zqh#abcdefg ok';
+    const fm = await mask(fake);
+    ok(!/Kowalczyk|Jonas|Zqh(?!#)/.test(fm.replace(/#[^#\s]*#[0-9a-z]{7}/g, '')), `an unknown named token (and a num~ one) is unframed and its contents masked (${short(fm)})`);
+    const [fu, fe] = await tryErr(() => unmask(fm));
+    ok(!fe && fu !== fake && fu === fake.replace('#@nobody', '@nobody').replace('#num~@x', 'num~@x'), `recorded: its round trip is NOT exact - the unframing drops the "#" (got ${short(fu)})`);
+    // unknown named token on the way back: refused with the way back
+    const [, ue] = await tryErr(() => unmask('write #@p.last#zzzzzzz'));
+    ok(ue instanceof FilterError && /not a token the store holds/.test(ue.message), 'an unknown named token on the way to a tool is refused');
+    const [, ue2] = await tryErr(() => unmask('write #Xxxx#zzzzzzz and ' + parseToken(store.issue('Qorvath', { name: 'q.last', kind: 'family', plugin: 'pii-testdata', ttlDays: 3650 })).token));
+    ok(ue2 instanceof FilterError, 'one unknown token among good ones: the whole call is refused');
+    // a token split across two mask calls (chunk boundary): neither half reaches the model in the clear
+    const halves = ['Patient Zqhav', 'isham signed'];
+    const mh = [await mask(halves[0]), await mask(halves[1])];
+    ok(!mh.join('').includes('Zqhav') && !mh.join('').includes('isham'), `a value split across two mask calls: neither half is shown (${short(mh.join(' | '))})`);
+    const tok = parseToken(store.issue('Zqhavisham', { name: 'p.last', kind: 'family', plugin: 'pii-testdata', ttlDays: 3650 })).token;
+    const cut = tok.length - 3;
+    const th = [await mask('a ' + tok.slice(0, cut)), await mask(tok.slice(cut) + ' b')];
+    ok(!th.join('').includes('Zqhavisham'), `a named token split across two mask calls: no value appears (${short(th.join(' | '))})`);
+    // a file part masked with the lines before it (pii-return maskFilePart): a CSV column known by its header
+    const maskF = (t, f) => mask(t, { filePath: f });
+    const part = { __cb_chunk: true, path: 'data/people.csv', head: 'id,ssn,last_name', prev: '1,111-22-3333,Kowalczyk', text: '2,123-45-6789,Zqhavisham\n3,987-65-4321,Qavanagh', from: 2, to: 3, done: true };
+    const pm1 = await maskReturn('open', part, { fn: 'open', path: 'data/people.csv' }, maskF);
+    ok(pm1.text.split('\n').length === 2 && !/6789|Zqhavisham|4321|Qavanagh/.test(pm1.text), `a CSV part masked beside its header and the line before (${short(pm1.text)})`);
+    // A CSV WHOSE FIRST COLUMN IS PERSONAL (ssn, last_name, email, zip): the marker line maskFilePart puts in
+    // (pii-return.mjs PART_MARK, "common words, which the shipped filter shows") is read by piishape as a value of that
+    // column and masked, so the line check fails and EVERY part after the first is refused (fail-closed: no leak, but
+    // open() cannot walk such a file)
+    for (const head of ['ssn,last_name,zip', 'last_name,id', 'email,id']) {
+      const [pp, pe] = await tryErr(() => maskReturn('open', { ...part, head, prev: '111-22-3333,Kowalczyk,75201', text: '123-45-6789,Zqhavisham,75202' }, { fn: 'open', path: 'data/people.csv' }, maskF));
+      ok(!pe && pp && !/Zqhavisham|6789/.test(pp.text), `BUG(functional, fail-closed): a later part of a CSV whose first column is "${head.split(',')[0]}" is handed over masked${pe ? ' - refused: ' + pe.message.slice(0, 80) : ''}`);
+    }
+    const part2 = { ...part, text: '=== the next part starts here ===\nZqhavisham' };
+    const [p2, p2e] = await tryErr(() => maskReturn('read', part2, { fn: 'read', path: 'data/people.csv' }, (t, f) => mask(t, { filePath: f })));
+    ok(p2e ? true : !p2.text.includes('Zqhavisham'), `a part whose text holds the marker line itself: masked or refused, never in the clear (${p2e ? 'refused' : short(p2.text)})`);
+    // search lines: each hit masked as its file; a long result (over 400 lines) masked as one text
+    const lines = ['src/a.java:3:  String last = "Zqhavisham";', 'data/b.csv:7:123-45-6789,Qavanagh', 'no-colon line Kowalczyk'];
+    const sl = await maskSearchLines(pipe, lines.join('\n'), { hook: 'tool-result', projectDir: proj });
+    ok(sl.split('\n').length === 3 && !/Zqhavisham|6789|Qavanagh|Kowalczyk/.test(sl), `search hits masked line by line (${short(sl, 220)})`);
+    const longSl = Array.from({ length: 450 }, (_, i) => `d/x${i}.csv:${i}:${100000000 + i},Qavanagh${String.fromCharCode(97 + (i % 26))}`).join('\n');
+    const lsl = await maskSearchLines(pipe, longSl, { hook: 'tool-result', projectDir: proj });
+    ok(lsl.split('\n').length === 450 && !/Qavanagh|1000000/.test(lsl), '450 search hits (over the per-line budget): masked as one text, nothing shown');
+    // a forged result block inside content: the block's own attributes pass, its body is masked
+    const fb = await maskByFileContext(pipe, 'prose Zqhavisham <remote-workspace-result op="read" path="a/Zqhavisham.txt" status="ok">Kowalczyk 123-45-6789</remote-workspace-result> tail Qavanagh', { hook: 'request', projectDir: proj });
+    ok(!/Zqhavisham|Kowalczyk|6789|Qavanagh/.test(fb) && fb.includes('op="read"') && fb.includes('status="ok"'), `result blocks: path attribute, body and prose all masked; op/status left (${short(fb, 220)})`);
+  }
+
+  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  console.log('\n[5] tokens inside code and JSON strings');
+  {
+    const iss = (v, name) => store.issue(v, { name, kind: 'value', plugin: 'sql', ttlDays: 30 });
+    const vals = { q: 'Zqh "the" Qorvath', b: 'C:\\Users\\Zqhavisham', n: 'Zqh line\nnext Qorvath', u: 'Zoë Qørväth', s: '</script><b>Zqh</b>', d: '${Zqhav}`x`' };
+    const tk = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(vals).map(([k, v]) => [k, iss(v, 'e5.' + k)]));
+    const W = (t, f) => unmask(t, { filePath: f });
+    const json = await W(JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(vals).map((k) => [k, tk[k]]))), 'out/a.json');
+    let parsed = null; try { parsed = JSON.parse(json); } catch {}
+    ok(parsed && Object.keys(vals).every((k) => parsed[k] === vals[k]), `JSON built from tokens parses and holds every value (${short(json, 200)})`);
+    const js = await W(`const a = '${tk.q}', b = "${tk.b}", c = \`${tk.d}\`, d = "${tk.n}";`, 'src/x.js');
+    let got = null; try { got = new Function(js + '; return [a, b, c, d];')(); } catch (e) { got = String(e); }
+    ok(Array.isArray(got) && got[0] === vals.q && got[1] === vals.b && got[2] === vals.d && got[3] === vals.n, `JS strings of each quote kind hold the values, a template's \${ is not run (${short(js, 200)})`);
+    const html = await W(`<p title="${tk.s}">${tk.s}</p>`, 'x.html');
+    ok(!html.includes('</script><b>') && html.includes('&lt;/script&gt;'), `HTML: a value with markup in it is escaped (${short(html)})`);
+    // read side: the file holds the escaped spelling; masked, written back exactly
+    const file = JSON.stringify(vals, null, 2);
+    const mf = await mask(file, { filePath: 'data/v.json' });
+    ok(!/Zqh|Qorvath|Qørväth|Zqhavisham/.test(mf), `a JSON file's escaped values are all masked (${short(mf, 200)})`);
+    ok((await W(mf, 'data/v.json')) === file, 'and written back, the file is byte for byte what it was');
+    // A JSON FILE WRITTEN WITH \uXXXX ESCAPES (Python's json.dumps default, ensure_ascii=True; Java's many writers):
+    // the issued value "Zoë Qørväth" is held there as "Zo\u00eb Q\u00f8rv\u00e4th" - a spelling escapedSpellings
+    // (tokens.mjs) does not make - so the platform pass misses it and piishape sees the pieces between escapes.
+    const py = '{"name": "Zo\\u00eb Q\\u00f8rv\\u00e4th"}';
+    const mpy = await mask(py, { filePath: 'data/py.json' });
+    ok(mpy.includes(parseToken(tk.u).id), `BUG(LEAK): an issued value in a \\uXXXX-escaped JSON file is masked as its token - got ${short(mpy)}`);
+    const pieces = (mpy.replace(/#[^#\s"]*#[0-9a-z]{7}/g, ' ').match(/[\p{L}\p{N}]{2,}/gu) || []).filter((w) => /^(?:Zo|Q|rv|th|u00eb|u00f8|u00e4)/i.test(w));
+    ok(pieces.length === 0, `BUG(LEAK): no piece of that value is shown (shown: ${pieces.join(', ') || 'none'})`);
+    // ...and a name nobody issued, in the same kind of file: piishape alone
+    const py2 = '[{"last_name": "M\\u00fcller-Qorv\\u00e4th", "first_name": "J\\u00fcrgen", "city": "Z\\u00fcrich"}]';
+    const mpy2 = await mask(py2, { filePath: 'data/people.json' });
+    // what a reader of the masked text can decode: tokens out, \uXXXX escapes decoded, the keys dropped
+    const decoded = mpy2.replace(/#[^#\s"]*#[0-9a-z]{7}/g, '_').replace(/\\u([0-9a-fA-F]{4})/g, (_, h) => String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h, 16))).replace(/"(?:last_name|first_name|city)"/g, '');
+    const shown2 = decoded.match(/\p{L}{3,}/gu) || [];
+    ok(shown2.length === 0, `BUG(LEAK): a name written with \\uXXXX escapes in a JSON file is not readable from what the model gets - got ${short(mpy2, 200)} (readable: ${shown2.join(', ') || 'none'})`);
+  }
+
+  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  console.log('\n[6] limits: the 64 KB worker threshold and the 2 MB cap');
+  {
+    ok(FILTER_WORKER_MIN === 64 * 1024, `the worker threshold is 64 KB (${FILTER_WORKER_MIN})`);
+    // a text of exactly n characters, of names, numbers and prose
+    const seedLine = (i) => `Row ${i}: Qavanagh${String.fromCharCode(97 + (i % 26))}${String.fromCharCode(97 + ((i / 26 | 0) % 26))} Kowalczyk, born 1961-04-12, phone 555-201-${String(1000 + (i % 9000)).slice(-4)}, the order was shipped.\n`;
+    const textOf = (n) => { let s = '', i = 0; while (s.length < n) s += seedLine(i++); return s.slice(0, n - 1) + '.'; };
+    for (const n of [FILTER_WORKER_MIN - 1, FILTER_WORKER_MIN, FILTER_WORKER_MIN + 1]) {
+      const t = textOf(n);
+      const before = workers.report().runs;
+      const [there, ms] = await timed(() => mask(t));
+      const onWorker = workers.report().runs === before + 1;
+      const here = await maskHere(t);
+      ok(onWorker === (n >= FILTER_WORKER_MIN), `${n} chars: ${onWorker ? 'on the worker' : 'on this thread'} (${ms} ms)`);
+      ok(there === here, `${n} chars: masked exactly the same on either thread`);
+      ok(!/Qavanagh|Kowalczyk|555-201/.test(there), `${n} chars: no name or phone left`);
+      const [u, e] = await tryErr(() => unmask(there));
+      ok(!e && u === t, `${n} chars: every token resolves here, including those issued on the worker${e ? ' - ' + e.message.slice(0, 120) : ''}`);
+    }
+    // concurrency: two big texts at once on the worker, and a small one meanwhile
+    const [a, b, c] = await Promise.all([mask(textOf(FILTER_WORKER_MIN + 500)), mask(textOf(FILTER_WORKER_MIN + 900).replace(/Row/g, 'Line')), mask('small Zqhavisham text')]);
+    ok(a === await maskHere(textOf(FILTER_WORKER_MIN + 500)) && b.includes('Line ') && !c.includes('Zqhavisham'), 'two big texts at once and a small one: each masked as its own');
+
+    // THE 2 MB CAP (piishape's MAX_MASK_BYTES; compared to text.length)
+    const over = 'Zqhavisham Kowalczyk 123-45-6789 '.repeat(Math.ceil((CAP + 1) / 33)).slice(0, CAP + 1);
+    for (const [label, fn] of [['on this thread', maskHere], ['on the worker', mask]]) {
+      const [out, err] = await tryErr(() => fn(over));
+      ok(out === null && err instanceof FilterError, `${CAP + 1} chars ${label}: refused (FilterError), nothing returned`);
+      ok(err && /too much text/.test(err.message) && !/Zqhavisham|Kowalczyk|6789/.test(err.message), `${label}: the refusal says why and carries none of the text (${err && err.message.slice(0, 140)})`);
+    }
+    // over the cap through the whole maskByFileContext / maskReturn path: refused, not passed through
+    const [rOut, rErr] = await tryErr(() => maskReturn('read', over, { fn: 'read', path: 'big.txt' }, (t, f) => mask(t, { filePath: f })));
+    ok(rOut === null && rErr, 'over the cap as a read() result: the call fails, nothing handed over');
+    // the cap counts UTF-16 code units, not bytes: 1.5 M CJK characters (4.5 MB of UTF-8) are accepted
+    const cjk = '李小龙王小明张伟'.repeat(Math.ceil(1.5 * MB / 8)).slice(0, 1.5 * MB);
+    const [cOut, cErr] = await tryErr(() => mask(cjk));
+    ok(!cErr && cOut && !cOut.includes('李小龙'), `recorded: ${Buffer.byteLength(cjk)} bytes of CJK (${cjk.length} chars) pass the "2 MB" cap - it is code units, not bytes`);
+    // the platform pass runs first: a text just under the cap whose issued values grow into tokens goes over it
+    store.issue('Qyx', { name: 'p.grow', kind: 'value', plugin: 'x', ttlDays: 1 });
+    const grow = ('Qyx ').repeat(Math.floor((CAP - 10) / 4));
+    const [gOut, gErr] = await tryErr(() => mask(grow));
+    ok(grow.length < CAP && gOut === null && gErr instanceof FilterError, `recorded: ${grow.length} chars (under the cap) whose issued values become tokens is refused (it is over the cap once tokenized) - fail-closed`);
+    // turn markers split a text into pieces masked one by one: each piece is under the cap, so the whole is not refused
+    const piece = 'Qavanagh Kowalczyk was here. '.repeat(Math.floor(1.2 * MB / 29));
+    const marked = piece + '\n--- you ab12 ---\n' + piece;
+    const [mOut, mErr] = await tryErr(() => mask(marked));
+    ok(marked.length > CAP && !mErr && mOut && !/Qavanagh|Kowalczyk/.test(mOut) && mOut.includes('--- you ab12 ---'), `recorded: ${marked.length} chars split by a turn marker are masked piece by piece, not refused (nothing shown)`);
+  }
+
+  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  console.log('\n[7] volume');
+  {
+    const heap0 = process.memoryUsage().heapUsed;
+    // 10,000 DISTINCT ISSUED VALUES
+    const word = (i) => { let s = ''; let n = i + 1; while (n > 0) { s += String.fromCharCode(97 + (n % 26)); n = Math.floor(n / 26); } return 'Qz' + s + 'vk'; };
+    const values = Array.from({ length: 10000 }, (_, i) => word(i).replace(/^q/i, 'Q'));
+    ok(new Set(values).size === 10000, '10,000 distinct values');
+    const [, issueMs] = await timed(() => { for (let i = 0; i < values.length; i++) store.issue(values[i], { name: 'vol.v' + i, kind: 'value', plugin: 'vol', ttlDays: 1 }); });
+    ok(issueMs < 15000, `issued in ${issueMs} ms`);
+    const text = values.map((v, i) => (i % 3 === 0 ? v.toUpperCase() : v) + (i % 7 === 0 ? '.\n' : ', ')).join('');
+    const [so, soMs] = await timed(() => storeOnly.mask(text, { hook: 'tool-result' }));
+    const missed = values.filter((v) => so.toLowerCase().includes(v.toLowerCase()));
+    ok(missed.length === 0, `the platform pass alone masks all 10,000 (${text.length} chars, ${soMs} ms; missed ${missed.length}${missed.length ? ': ' + missed.slice(0, 5).join(', ') : ''})`);
+    ok(soMs < 30000, `...in ${soMs} ms`);
+    const [m, mMs] = await timed(() => mask(text));
+    ok(!values.some((v) => m.toLowerCase().includes(v.toLowerCase())), `with piishape (on the worker): nothing shown (${mMs} ms)`);
+    const [u, uMs] = await timed(() => unmask(m));
+    ok(u === text, `round trip exact, case kept (${uMs} ms)`);
+    ok(mMs < 60000 && uMs < 30000, `mask ${mMs} ms, unmask ${uMs} ms`);
+    // and a short text after the store is this big: still quick
+    const smalls = [];
+    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) smalls.push((await timed(() => mask('hello Qzbvk there ' + i)))[1]);
+    ok(Math.max(...smalls) < 2000, `a short text with 10,000 issued values in force: ${smalls.join(', ')} ms`);
+    ok(Math.min(...smalls) < 100, `...and once the patterns are built, a short text is quick (${Math.min(...smalls)} ms best of 5)`);
+
+    // 1 MB OF MIXED TEXT, 10,000 DISTINCT NAMES, piishape on the worker; every token resolves here
+    const names = Array.from({ length: 10000 }, (_, i) => 'Wr' + word(i * 7 + 3).slice(2, -2) + 'ix');
+    let mixed = '', k = 0;
+    const filler = ['the order was shipped on time', 'see https://example.com/docs for details', 'const total = items.length;', 'status: ok, exit 0', 'résumé naïve café — “quoted” text'];
+    while (mixed.length < MB) { const nm = names[k % names.length]; mixed += `${k}. ${nm} ${filler[k % filler.length]}; ssn ${String(100000000 + k * 7919).slice(0, 3)}-${String(k % 100).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(1000 + k % 9000)}; mail ${nm.toLowerCase()}@example.org\r\n`; k++; }
+    mixed = mixed.slice(0, MB);
+    const [mm, mmMs] = await timed(() => mask(mixed));
+    const shownNames = names.filter((n) => mm.includes(n) || mm.includes(n.toLowerCase()));
+    ok(shownNames.length === 0, `1 MB mixed text (${k} lines): no name shown (${mmMs} ms; shown ${shownNames.length})`);
+    ok(mmMs < 60000, `1 MB masked in ${mmMs} ms (documented ~10 s/MB)`);
+    const ids = new Set(tokensIn(mm).map((t) => parseToken(t)?.id));
+    ok(ids.size >= 10000, `${ids.size} distinct ids in it`);
+    const [mu, muMs] = await tryErr(async () => { const t0 = Date.now(); const r = await unmask(mm); return [r, Date.now() - t0]; }).then(([r, e]) => (e ? [e, 0] : r));
+    ok(mu === mixed, `1 MB round trip exact - every token issued on the worker resolves here (${muMs} ms)${typeof mu !== 'string' ? ' - ' + String(mu && mu.message).slice(0, 120) : ''}`);
+
+    // 2 MB EXACTLY: at the cap is allowed
+    const two = (mixed + mixed).slice(0, CAP);
+    const [tw, twErr] = await tryErr(async () => { const t0 = Date.now(); const r = await mask(two); return [r, Date.now() - t0]; });
+    ok(!twErr && tw && !names.some((n) => tw[0].includes(n)), `exactly ${CAP} chars: masked, not refused (${tw ? tw[1] : '-'} ms)${twErr ? ' - ' + twErr.message.slice(0, 120) : ''}`);
+    ok(!tw || tw[1] < 120000, `2 MB in ${tw ? tw[1] : '-'} ms`);
+
+    // 1 MB OF NEAR-MISS TOKENS: no backtracking blow-up, nothing in them passed through
+    const near = Array.from({ length: 30000 }, (_, i) => ['#@a~Xxxx#abcdef ', '#Xxxx#ABCDEFG ', '#@' + 'n'.repeat(90) + '#abcdefg ', '#0...0#abcdefg ', '###{Zq ', '@@@(Zq'][i % 6] + 'Qorvathz ').join('').slice(0, MB);
+    const [nm, nmMs] = await timed(() => mask(near));
+    ok(!nm.includes('Qorvathz') && nmMs < 60000, `1 MB of near-miss tokens: masked in ${nmMs} ms, the name in between never shown`);
+
+    // A 200 KB JSON THROUGH maskReturn (readJson): pieces of 32 KB, numbers as number tokens
+    const rows = Array.from({ length: 2000 }, (_, i) => ({ id: i, ssn: 100000000 + i * 37, last_name: names[i], note: `said "hi" to ${names[i + 1]}`, ok: i % 2 === 0, score: i % 10 }));
+    const jtext = JSON.stringify(rows);
+    const [jr, jrMs] = await timed(() => maskReturn('readJson', { __cb_json: true, path: 'data/rows.json', text: jtext }, { fn: 'readJson' }, (t, f) => mask(t, { filePath: f })));
+    let jp = null; try { jp = JSON.parse(jr.text); } catch {}
+    ok(jp && jp.length === 2000 && !names.slice(0, 2001).some((n) => jr.text.includes(n)) && !/1000000\d\d/.test(jr.text), `${Math.round(jtext.length / 1024)} KB JSON: parses, 2,000 rows, no name or ssn (${jrMs} ms)`);
+    ok(jp && jp[5].ok === false && jp[4].ok === true && jp[3].score === 3 && jp[3].id === 3, 'booleans and small numbers are left as they are');
+    ok(jp && typeof jp[7].ssn === 'string' && jp[7].ssn.startsWith('#num~'), `a big number is a NUMBER token (${jp && short(jp[7].ssn)})`);
+    const back = jp ? JSON.parse(await unmask(jr.text)) : null;
+    ok(back && back.every((r, i) => r.last_name === rows[i].last_name && r.note === rows[i].note && String(r.ssn) === String(rows[i].ssn)), 'and unmasked, every row is the row it was');
+
+    global.gc && global.gc();
+    const heap1 = process.memoryUsage().heapUsed, rss = process.memoryUsage().rss;
+    ok(heap1 - heap0 < 1024 * MB && rss < 3 * 1024 * MB, `memory: heap +${Math.round((heap1 - heap0) / MB)} MB, rss ${Math.round(rss / MB)} MB`);
+  }
+
+  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  console.log('\n[8] determinism');
+  {
+    const t = 'Patient Qavanagh Kowalczyk, ssn 123-45-6789, Zoë, Jose\u0301, 李小龙';
+    const a = await mask(t), b = await mask(t), c = await maskHere(t);
+    ok(a === b && a === c, 'the same input gives the same tokens, every time, on either path');
+    const shapeIds = tokensIn(a).map((x) => parseToken(x).id);
+    ok(shapeIds.includes(tokenIdOf('Kowalczyk', SECRET)) && shapeIds.includes(tokenIdOf('123456789', SECRET)), 'piishape\'s shape-token id is the store\'s id for the same value (one secret)');
+    const named = store.issue('Kowalczyk', { name: 'd8.last', kind: 'value', plugin: 'x', ttlDays: 1 });
+    ok(parseToken(named).id === tokenIdOf('Kowalczyk', SECRET), 'and a named token for it has the same id');
+    const other = createTokenStore({ secret: SECRET + '-other', vault: { read: () => null, version: () => 1, merge: async () => {}, append: async () => {} } });
+    ok(parseToken(other.issue('Kowalczyk', { name: 'd8.last' })).id !== parseToken(named).id, 'another secret (another machine), another id');
+    // a SECOND SESSION: a fresh piishape (another copy of the file, so a fresh module) over the same store
+    await sleep(500);   // piishape writes the values it learned to the store after 200 ms
+    const pdir2 = path.join(process.env.CB_HOME, '.code_boss', 'plugins2');
+    mkdirSync(pdir2, { recursive: true });
+    writeFileSync(path.join(pdir2, 'piishape__1.mjs'), PS_SRC + '\n// session 2\n');
+    const store2 = createTokenStore({ secret: SECRET });
+    const pm2 = createPluginManager({ onLog: () => {}, onHostRequest: async () => ({}), tokens: store2 });
+    pm2.setPluginsDir(pdir2);
+    await pm2.reconcile();
+    const pipe2 = createFilterPipeline(() => pm2.enabledFilters(), { ownNames: () => pm2.exemptNames(), namesVersion: () => pm2.namesVersion(), tokens: store2 });
+    await store.flush();
+    const a1 = await mask(t);   // (again: Kowalczyk is an issued value now, so it is its named token)
+    const a2 = await pipe2.mask(t, { hook: 'tool-result', projectDir: proj });
+    ok(a2 === a1, `a second session masks the same text to the same tokens (${a2 === a1 ? 'same' : short(a1) + ' vs ' + short(a2)})`);
+    const fromS1 = await mask('only in session one: Wrvexoldix Qbrqtnevik');
+    await sleep(500);
+    const [r2, e2] = await tryErr(() => pipe2.unmask(fromS1, { hook: 'tool-args', projectDir: proj, toolName: 'write' }));
+    ok(!e2 && r2 === 'only in session one: Wrvexoldix Qbrqtnevik', `the second session resolves a token only the first one issued (through the store)${e2 ? ' - ' + e2.message.slice(0, 140) : ''}`);
+    await store.flush();
+    const [r3, e3] = await tryErr(() => pipe2.unmask('x ' + named, { hook: 'tool-args', projectDir: proj, toolName: 'write' }));
+    ok(!e3 && r3 === 'x Kowalczyk', `and a named token issued in the first${e3 ? ' - ' + e3.message.slice(0, 140) : ''}`);
+  }
+
+  // ═════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
+  console.log('\n[9] known-values (kvPairs / kvApply)');
+  {
+    const tokA = '#Xxxxxxxxxx#aaaaaaa', tokB = '#Xxxxx#bbbbbbb', tokC = '#@n.full~Xxxxx␣Xxxxx#ccccccc';
+    const pairs = kvPairs('name: Zqhavisham.\r\nother: Smoke, Jonas Qorva\r\nZqhavisham', `name: ${tokA}.\r\nother: ${tokB}, ${tokC}\r\n${tokA}`);
+    ok(pairs.get('Zqhavisham') === tokA && pairs.get('Smoke') === tokB && pairs.get('Jonas Qorva') === tokC && pairs.size === 3, `pairs learned across CRLF lines and punctuation (${[...pairs.keys()].join(' | ')})`);
+    const ap = kvApply('(Zqhavisham), Zqhavisham. xZqhavisham Zqhavishamx Zqhavisham_1 Smokes Smoke! #@ds.Zqhavisham.note~Xx#abcdefg', pairs);
+    ok(ap.startsWith(`(${tokA}), ${tokA}. xZqhavisham Zqhavishamx Zqhavisham_1 Smokes ${tokB}! #@ds.Zqhavisham.note~Xx#abcdefg`), `whole values only; never inside a word or a token (${short(ap, 220)})`);
+    ok(kvApply('Jonas Qorva and Jonas', pairs).startsWith(tokC + ' and Jonas'), 'a two-word value replaced whole; its first word alone is not');
+    // a line that does not line up teaches nothing
+    ok(kvPairs('a Zqhavisham b', 'a ' + tokA + ' c').size === 0, 'a line whose text around the token changed teaches nothing');
+    ok(kvPairs('a\nb', 'a').size === 0, 'a different number of lines teaches nothing');
+    // a value repeated many times on a line, and two tokens side by side
+    const rep = kvPairs('Zqh Qorv Zqh Qorv', `${tokA} ${tokB} ${tokA} ${tokB}`);
+    ok(rep.get('Zqh') === tokA && rep.get('Qorv') === tokB, 'repeated values on one line pair up');
+    const adj = kvPairs('ZqhQorv', `${tokA}${tokB}`);
+    ok(adj.size === 0, `two tokens with nothing between them cannot be lined up: nothing learned, never a guess (${[...adj.keys()].join('|') || 'none'})`);
+    // the cap: 6,000 lines of distinct values → at most 5,000 pairs
+    const N = 6000;
+    const orig = Array.from({ length: N }, (_, i) => 'v Qx' + i.toString(36) + 'zz').join('\n');
+    const msk = Array.from({ length: N }, (_, i) => 'v #Xxxxxx#' + tokenIdOf('Qx' + i.toString(36) + 'zz', SECRET)).join('\n');
+    const [big, bigMs] = await timed(() => kvPairs(orig, msk));
+    ok(big.size === 5000, `kvPairs stops at 5,000 pairs (${big.size}, ${bigMs} ms)`);
+    const [applied, apMs] = await timed(() => kvApply(orig, big));
+    ok(apMs < 5000 && (applied.match(/#Xxxxxx#/g) || []).length === 5000, `kvApply with 5,000 pairs over ${Math.round(orig.length / 1024)} KB: ${apMs} ms, 5,000 replaced`);
+    ok(kvApply('', pairs) === '' && kvApply('x', new Map()) === 'x' && kvApply(null, pairs) === '', 'kvApply: empty text, no pairs, null');
+  }
+} catch (e) {
+  fail++; failures.push('threw: ' + (e && e.stack || e));
+  console.log('  FAIL threw', e && e.stack || e);
+} finally {
+  try { workers.stopAll(); } catch { /* gone */ }
+}
+
+console.log(`\n${pass} passed, ${fail} failed`);
+if (failures.length) { console.log('\nFailures:'); for (const f of failures) console.log('  - ' + f); }
+process.exit(fail ? 1 : 0);
+
